const app = require('../app');
const { sequelize, Utileria } = require('../models');
const { createBaseFixtures, removeBaseFixtures } = require('./helpers/fixtures');
const { loginAgent, removeTestUser } = require('./helpers/auth');

let ids;
let agent;
let authUser;

beforeAll(async () => {
  await sequelize.authenticate();
  ids = await createBaseFixtures();
  ({ agent, user: authUser } = await loginAgent(app));
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await removeTestUser(authUser);
  await removeBaseFixtures(ids);
  await sequelize.close();

describe('utilerias', () => {
  test('GET /api/utilerias lista las utilerias (200)', async () => {
    const res = await agent.get('/api/utilerias');

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(
      res.body.data.some((utileria) => String(utileria.codigoUtileria) === String(ids.utileriaId)),
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  test('PUT /api/utilerias/:id actualiza estadoUtileria (200)', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .put(`/api/utilerias/${ids.utileriaId}`)
      .send({ estadoUtileria: false });

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(Boolean(res.body.data.estadoUtileria)).toBe(false);

    const enDb = await Utileria.findByPk(ids.utileriaId);
    expect(Boolean(enDb.estadoUtileria)).toBe(false);
  });

  test('PUT /api/utilerias/:id inexistente responde 404', async () => {
    const res = await agent.put('/api/utilerias/999999999').send({ estadoUtileria: true });

    expect(res.status).toBe(404);

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/backend/tests/vehiculos.test.js =====
const app = require('../app');
const {
  sequelize,
  Vehiculo,
  Lavado,
  EstadoLavado,
  CategoriaVehiculo,
  MarcaVehiculo,
  ModeloVehiculo,
} = require('../models');
const { ensureLavadoCatalogs } = require('../services/catalogSeedService');
const { createBaseFixtures, removeBaseFixtures, suffix } = require('./helpers/fixtures');
const { loginAgent, removeTestUser } = require('./helpers/auth');

let ids;
let agent;
let authUser;
let estadoEnEsperaId;
let estadoEntregadoId;
const createdVehiculos = [];
const createdCategorias = [];
const createdMarcas = [];

beforeAll(async () => {
  await sequelize.authenticate();
  ids = await createBaseFixtures();
  await ensureLavadoCatalogs();
  estadoEnEsperaId = (
    await EstadoLavado.findOne({ where: { descripcionEstado: 'En Espera' } })
  ).codigoEstado;
  estadoEntregadoId = (
    await EstadoLavado.findOne({ where: { descripcionEstado: 'Entregado' } })
  ).codigoEstado;
  ({ agent, user: authUser } = await loginAgent(app));
});

afterEach(async () => {
  while (createdVehiculos.length) {
    const id = createdVehiculos.pop();
    await Lavado.destroy({ where: { vehi: id } });
    await Vehiculo.destroy({ where: { codigoVehi: id } });
  }
  while (createdMarcas.length) {
    const id = createdMarcas.pop();
    await ModeloVehiculo.destroy({ where: { marcaVehi: id } });
    await MarcaVehiculo.destroy({ where: { codigoMarcaVehi: id } });
  }
  while (createdCategorias.length) {
    const id = createdCategorias.pop();
    await MarcaVehiculo.destroy({ where: { categoriaVehi: id } });
    await CategoriaVehiculo.destroy({ where: { codigoCategoriaVehi: id } });
  }
});

afterAll(async () => {
  await removeTestUser(authUser);
  await removeBaseFixtures(ids);
  await sequelize.close();
});

describe('CRUD de vehículos y catálogos', () => {
  test('POST /api/vehiculos crea un vehículo (201)', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `P${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });

    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    expect(res.body.data.codigoVehi).toBeDefined();
    createdVehiculos.push(res.body.data.codigoVehi);
  });

  test('POST /api/vehiculos con cliente inexistente responde 400', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: 'BAD001', estadoVehi: true, cliente: 999999999, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });

    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('POST /api/vehiculos con modelo inexistente responde 400', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: 'BAD002', estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: 999999999 });

    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('GET /api/vehiculos lista vehículos (200)', async () => {
    const res = await agent.get('/api/vehiculos');

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(Array.isArray(res.body.data)).toBe(true);
  });

  test('GET /api/vehiculos incluye marca y categoría del vehículo', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `M${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    createdVehiculos.push(create.body.data.codigoVehi);

    const res = await agent.get('/api/vehiculos');
    const vehiculo = res.body.data.find((v) => v.codigoVehi === create.body.data.codigoVehi);

    expect(vehiculo.modelo).toBeDefined();
    expect(vehiculo.modelo.marca).toBeDefined();
    expect(vehiculo.modelo.marca.categoria).toBeDefined();
  });

  test('GET /api/vehiculos?cliente filtra por cliente', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `F${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    createdVehiculos.push(create.body.data.codigoVehi);

    const res = await agent.get(`/api/vehiculos?cliente=${ids.clienteId}`);

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.every((v) => v.cliente === ids.clienteId)).toBe(true);
  });

  test('los vehículos inactivos se excluyen del listado pero se obtienen por id', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `I${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const vehiculoId = create.body.data.codigoVehi;
    createdVehiculos.push(vehiculoId);

    await agent.put(`/api/vehiculos/${vehiculoId}`).send({ estadoVehi: false });

    const listado = await agent.get('/api/vehiculos');
    expect(listado.body.data.some((v) => v.codigoVehi === vehiculoId)).toBe(false);

    const porId = await agent.get(`/api/vehiculos/${vehiculoId}`);
    expect(porId.status).toBe(200);
    expect(porId.body.data.codigoVehi).toBe(vehiculoId);
  });

  test('GET /api/vehiculos/:id inexistente responde 404', async () => {
    const res = await agent.get('/api/vehiculos/999999999');

    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });

  test('PUT /api/vehiculos/:id actualiza (200)', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `E${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const id = create.body.data.codigoVehi;
    createdVehiculos.push(id);

    const res = await agent.put(`/api/vehiculos/${id}`).send({ patenteVehi: 'NUEVA123' });

    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.body.data.patenteVehi).toBe('NUEVA123');
  });

  test('DELETE /api/vehiculos/:id responde 204', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `D${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const id = create.body.data.codigoVehi;

    const res = await agent.delete(`/api/vehiculos/${id}`);

    expect(res.status).toBe(204);
    expect(await Vehiculo.findByPk(id)).toBeNull();
  });

  test('DELETE /api/vehiculos/:id inexistente responde 404', async () => {
    const res = await agent.delete('/api/vehiculos/999999999');

    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });

  test('DELETE /api/vehiculos/:id con lavados asociados aplica baja lógica (204)', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `C${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const vehiculoId = create.body.data.codigoVehi;
    createdVehiculos.push(vehiculoId);

    await Lavado.create({
      fechaHoraLavado: new Date(),
      estado: ids.estadoLavadoId,
      utileria: ids.utileriaId,
      vehi: vehiculoId,
      categoriaLavado: ids.categoriaLavadoId,
      login: ids.loginId,
    });

    const res = await agent.delete(`/api/vehiculos/${vehiculoId}`);

    expect(res.status).toBe(204);

    const enDb = await Vehiculo.findByPk(vehiculoId);
    expect(enDb).not.toBeNull();
    expect(Boolean(enDb.estadoVehi)).toBe(false);
  });

  test('DELETE /api/vehiculos/:id con lavados en otro estado aplica baja lógica (204)', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `N${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const vehiculoId = create.body.data.codigoVehi;
    createdVehiculos.push(vehiculoId);

    await Lavado.create({
      fechaHoraLavado: new Date(),
      estado: ids.estadoLavadoAltId,
      utileria: ids.utileriaId,
      vehi: vehiculoId,
      categoriaLavado: ids.categoriaLavadoId,
      login: ids.loginId,
    });

    const res = await agent.delete(`/api/vehiculos/${vehiculoId}`);

    expect(res.status).toBe(204);

    const enDb = await Vehiculo.findByPk(vehiculoId);
    expect(enDb).not.toBeNull();
    expect(Boolean(enDb.estadoVehi)).toBe(false);
  });

  test('PUT /api/vehiculos/:id con lavado activo responde 409 y no modifica', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `B${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const vehiculoId = create.body.data.codigoVehi;
    createdVehiculos.push(vehiculoId);

    await Lavado.create({
      estado: estadoEnEsperaId,
      utileria: ids.utileriaId,
      vehi: vehiculoId,
      categoriaLavado: ids.categoriaLavadoId,
      login: ids.loginId,
    });

    const res = await agent.put(`/api/vehiculos/${vehiculoId}`).send({ patenteVehi: 'CAMBIO01' });

    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    const enDb = await Vehiculo.findByPk(vehiculoId);
    expect(enDb.patenteVehi).not.toBe('CAMBIO01');
  });

  test('DELETE /api/vehiculos/:id con lavado activo responde 409 y no da de baja', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `L${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const vehiculoId = create.body.data.codigoVehi;
    createdVehiculos.push(vehiculoId);

    await Lavado.create({
      estado: estadoEnEsperaId,
      utileria: ids.utileriaId,
      vehi: vehiculoId,
      categoriaLavado: ids.categoriaLavadoId,
      login: ids.loginId,
    });

    const res = await agent.delete(`/api/vehiculos/${vehiculoId}`);

    expect(res.status).toBe(409);
    const enDb = await Vehiculo.findByPk(vehiculoId);
    expect(enDb).not.toBeNull();
    expect(Boolean(enDb.estadoVehi)).toBe(true);
  });

  test('DELETE /api/vehiculos/:id con lavado entregado aplica baja lógica (204)', async () => {
    const create = await agent
      .post('/api/vehiculos')
      .send({ patenteVehi: `T${suffix()}`, estadoVehi: true, cliente: ids.clienteId, modeloVehi: ids.modeloVehiculoId });
    const vehiculoId = create.body.data.codigoVehi;
    createdVehiculos.push(vehiculoId);

    await Lavado.create({
      estado: estadoEntregadoId,
      utileria: ids.utileriaId,
      vehi: vehiculoId,
      categoriaLavado: ids.categoriaLavadoId,
      login: ids.loginId,
    });

    const res = await agent.delete(`/api/vehiculos/${vehiculoId}`);

    expect(res.status).toBe(204);
    const enDb = await Vehiculo.findByPk(vehiculoId);
    expect(enDb).not.toBeNull();
    expect(Boolean(enDb.estadoVehi)).toBe(false);
  });

  test('POST /api/categorias-vehiculo crea una categoría (201)', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/categorias-vehiculo')
      .send({ categoriaVehi: `Cat ${suffix()}` });

    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    createdCategorias.push(res.body.data.codigoCategoriaVehi);
  });

  test('POST /api/marcas-vehiculo con categoría inexistente responde 400', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/marcas-vehiculo')
      .send({ marcaVehi: 'MarcaX', categoriaVehi: 999999999 });

    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
  });

  test('POST /api/modelos-vehiculo crea un modelo (201)', async () => {
    const res = await agent
      .post('/api/modelos-vehiculo')
      .send({ modeloVehi: `Modelo ${suffix()}`, marcaVehi: ids.marcaVehiculoId });

    expect(res.status).toBe(201);
    await ModeloVehiculo.destroy({ where: { codigoModeloVehi: res.body.data.codigoModeloVehi } });
  });
});

class AppError extends Error {
  constructor(message, statusCode = 400) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'AppError';
    this.statusCode = statusCode;
    this.isOperational = true;
  }
}

module.exports = AppError;

function asyncHandler(fn) {
  return (req, res, next) => Promise.resolve(fn(req, res, next)).catch(next);
}

module.exports = asyncHandler;

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/backend/utils/password.js =====
const crypto = require('crypto');

const KEY_LENGTH = 64;
const PREFIX = 'scrypt';

function hashPassword(password) {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
  const derived = crypto.scryptSync(password, salt, KEY_LENGTH).toString('hex');
  return `${PREFIX}$${salt}$${derived}`;
}

function verifyPassword(password, stored) {
  if (typeof stored !== 'string') {
    return false;
  }

  const parts = stored.split('$');
  if (parts.length !== 3 || parts[0] !== PREFIX) {
    return false;
  }

  const [, salt, hash] = parts;
  const derived = crypto.scryptSync(password, salt, KEY_LENGTH).toString('hex');
  const expected = Buffer.from(hash, 'hex');
  const actual = Buffer.from(derived, 'hex');

  if (expected.length !== actual.length) {
    return false;
  }

  return crypto.timingSafeEqual(expected, actual);
}

module.exports = { hashPassword, verifyPassword };

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/backend/utils/validators.js =====
const AppError = require('./AppError');

function isMissing(value) {
  return value === undefined || value === null || value === '';
}

function requireFields(payload, fields) {
  const missing = fields.filter((field) => isMissing(payload[field]));
  if (missing.length > 0) {
    throw new AppError(`Faltan campos obligatorios: ${missing.join(', ')}`, 400);
  }
}

async function assertExists(model, id, message) {
  if (isMissing(id)) {
    throw new AppError(message, 400);
  }
  const found = await model.findByPk(id);
  if (!found) {
    throw new AppError(message, 400);
  }
}

module.exports = { requireFields, assertExists };
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/backend/utils/validators.js =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/backend/app.js =====
const express = require('express');
const session = require('express-session');
const routes = require('./routes');
const notFound = require('./middlewares/notFound');
const errorHandler = require('./middlewares/errorHandler');
const { loadConfig } = require('./config');

const config = loadConfig();
const app = express();

app.use(express.json());
app.use(
  session({
    secret: config.session.secret,
    resave: false,
    saveUninitialized: false,
    cookie: {
      httpOnly: true,
      sameSite: 'lax',
      secure: config.session.cookieSecure,
    },
  }),
);

app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({ success: true, data: { status: 'ok' } });
});

app.use('/api', routes);

app.use(notFound);
app.use(errorHandler);

module.exports = app;

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/backend/server.js =====
const app = require('./app');
const { sequelize, config } = require('./config/database');
const { ensureDefaultAdmin } = require('./services/adminService');

async function start() {
  try {
    await sequelize.authenticate();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('No se pudo conectar a la base de datos:', error.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  try {
    await ensureDefaultAdmin();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('No se pudo garantizar la cuenta administrador:', error.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }

  app.listen(config.port, () => {
    console.log(`Backend escuchando en el puerto ${config.port}`);
  });
}

start();

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/components/AppHeader.jsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import userIcon from '../assets/usuario.png';
import BrandHeader from './BrandHeader.jsx';
import LogoutPopup from './LogoutPopup.jsx';
import { logout } from '../services/authService.js';

export default function AppHeader() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [showLogout, setShowLogout] = useState(false);

  async function handleConfirmLogout() {
    try {
      await logout();
    } finally {
      navigate('/login', { replace: true });
    }
  }

  return (
    <header className="flex items-center gap-3">
      <BrandHeader />

      <div className="ml-auto mr-6">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowLogout(true)}
          className="flex flex-col items-center"
        >
          <img src={userIcon} alt="Usuario" className="h-12 w-12" />
          <span className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-brand-navy">ADMIN</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {showLogout ? (
        <LogoutPopup onConfirm={handleConfirmLogout} onClose={() => setShowLogout(false)} />
      ) : null}
    </header>
  );
}

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/components/ClienteVehiculoInfo.jsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import autoIcon from '../assets/auto.png';
import userIcon from '../assets/usuario.png';
import BrandHeader from './BrandHeader.jsx';

function InfoRow({ label, value }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-4">
      <dt className="w-24 text-sm font-bold text-gray-300">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{value}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}

const buttonBase = 'rounded-full px-4 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-white';

export default function ClienteVehiculoInfo({
  cliente,
  vehiculos = [],
  onNuevoVehiculo,
  onEditarCliente,
  onEliminarCliente,
  onEditarVehiculo,
  onEliminarVehiculo,
  onLavar,
  onVolver,
}) {
  const [hoveredId, setHoveredId] = useState(null);

  function cardClasses(cardId) {
    const base = 'transition-transform duration-200';
    if (hoveredId === null) {
      return base;
    }
    return `${base} ${hoveredId === cardId ? 'z-10 scale-105' : 'scale-95'}`;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <BrandHeader />

      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-5xl rounded-3xl border-3 border-blue-900 bg-[#8f8a8a] p-6">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-6">
          <div
            className={cardClasses('cliente')}
            onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredId('cliente')}
            onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredId(null)}
            data-testid="cliente-card"
          >
            <div className="w-100 rounded-2xl bg-[#2637a2] p-6">
              <div className="mt-1 mb-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onNuevoVehiculo}
                  className={`${buttonBase} w-30 h-12 bg-green-600`}
                >
                  + VEHICULO
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center mb-8 gap-4">
                <img src={userIcon} alt="Usuario" className="h-14 w-14" />
                <dl className="space-y-1">
                  <InfoRow label="DNI" value={cliente?.dni} />
                  <InfoRow label="NOMBRE" value={cliente?.nombre} />
                  <InfoRow label="APELLIDO" value={cliente?.apellido} />
                  <InfoRow label="TELEFONO" value={cliente?.celular} />
                  <InfoRow label="e-m@il" value={cliente?.email} />
                </dl>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onEditarCliente}
                  className={`${buttonBase} bg-gray-400`}
                >
                  Editar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={onEliminarCliente}
                  className={`${buttonBase} bg-red-600`}
                >
                  Eliminar
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {vehiculos.map((vehiculo) => (
            <div
              key={vehiculo.codigoVehi}
              className={cardClasses(`vehiculo-${vehiculo.codigoVehi}`)}
              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredId(`vehiculo-${vehiculo.codigoVehi}`)}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredId(null)}
              data-testid="vehiculo-card"
            >
              <div className="w-60 rounded-2xl bg-[#2637a2] p-6">
                <div className="mb-1 flex justify-end gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onLavar(vehiculo)}
                    className={`${buttonBase} w-30 h-12 bg-green-600`}
                  >
                    LAVAR
                  </button>
                </div>
                <img src={autoIcon} alt="Vehiculo" className="h-10 w-10" />
                <dl className="mt-2 space-y-1">
                  <InfoRow
                    label="VEHICULO"
                    value={vehiculo.modelo?.marca?.categoria?.categoriaVehi}
                  />
                  <InfoRow label="MARCA" value={vehiculo.modelo?.marca?.marcaVehi} />
                  <InfoRow label="MODELO" value={vehiculo.modelo?.modeloVehi} />
                  <InfoRow label="PATENTE" value={vehiculo.patenteVehi} />
                </dl>
                <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onEditarVehiculo(vehiculo)}
                    className={`${buttonBase} bg-gray-400`}
                  >
                    Editar
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onEliminarVehiculo(vehiculo)}
                    className={`${buttonBase} bg-red-600`}
                  >
                    Eliminar
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onVolver}
            className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
          >
            ←
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/components/ClienteVehiculoInfo.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/components/ConfirmPopup.jsx =====
export default function ConfirmPopup({ title = '!!! AVISO !!!', message, onConfirm, onCancel }) {
  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/30 p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl bg-[#e0dede] p-8 text-center">
        <p className="text-lg font-bold text-red-700">{title}</p>
        <p className="mt-3 text-lg font-bold text-red-700">{message}</p>
        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-6">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onConfirm}
            className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-6 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-white"
          >
            ACEPTAR
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-6 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-white"
          >
            CANCELAR
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import ConfirmPopup from './ConfirmPopup.jsx';

describe('ConfirmPopup', () => {
  test('muestra el aviso y los botones, y dispara los callbacks', async () => {
    const onConfirm = vi.fn();
    const onCancel = vi.fn();
    const user = userEvent.setup();

    render(
      <ConfirmPopup
        message="¿ Desea eliminar permanentemente el Juan seleccionado ?"
        onConfirm={onConfirm}
        onCancel={onCancel}

      screen.getByText('¿ Desea eliminar permanentemente el Juan seleccionado ?'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' }));
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalled();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'CANCELAR' }));
    expect(onCancel).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/components/DateTimePanel.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { formatDate, formatTime } from '../utils/formatDateTime.js';

export default function DateTimePanel() {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 60000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const { hour, minute, period } = formatTime(now);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl bg-brand-panel px-10 py-14 text-center text-white">
      <p className="text-xl font-bold">{formatDate(now)}</p>
      <p className="mt-8">
        <span className="text-5xl font-bold">{hour}</span>
        <span className="text-3xl font-bold">  {minute}</span>
        <span className="ml-2 text-2xl font-bold">{period}</span>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/components/LavadoInfoPopup.jsx =====
function InfoRow({ label, value }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-4">
      <dt className="w-28 text-sm font-bold text-brand-navy">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-navy">{value ?? ''}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function LavadoInfoPopup({ lavado, onClose }) {
  const vehiculo = lavado.vehiculo ?? {};
  const modelo = vehiculo.modelo ?? {};
  const persona = vehiculo.datosCliente?.persona ?? {};

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
    >
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl border-2 border-gray-400 bg-brand-pink p-8">
        <h2 className="text-center text-xl font-bold text-brand-navy">Informacion del Lavado</h2>
        <dl className="mt-4 space-y-1">
          <InfoRow label="PATENTE" value={vehiculo.patenteVehi} />
          <InfoRow label="CATEGORIA" value={lavado.categoria?.descripcionLavado} />
          <InfoRow label="MODELO" value={modelo.modeloVehi} />
          <InfoRow label="MARCA" value={modelo.marca?.marcaVehi} />
          <InfoRow label="DNI" value={persona.dni} />
          <InfoRow label="NOMBRE" value={persona.nombre} />
          <InfoRow label="APELLIDO" value={persona.apellido} />
          <InfoRow label="TELEFONO" value={persona.celular} />
          <InfoRow label="e-m@il" value={persona.email} />
        </dl>
        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-full bg-brand-green px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
          >
            Aceptar
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/ClienteInfoPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import ClienteVehiculoInfo from '../components/ClienteVehiculoInfo.jsx';
import ConfirmPopup from '../components/ConfirmPopup.jsx';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { deleteCliente, getCliente } from '../services/clientService.js';
import { MENSAJE_LAVADO_ACTIVO, tieneLavadoActivo } from '../services/lavadoService.js';
import { deleteVehiculo, listByCliente } from '../services/vehicleService.js';

export default function ClienteInfoPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const origen = location.state?.from ?? '/clientes';
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [cliente, setCliente] = useState(null);
  const [vehiculos, setVehiculos] = useState([]);
  const [showDelete, setShowDelete] = useState(false);
  const [showDeleteVehiculo, setShowDeleteVehiculo] = useState(null);
  const [showLavarWarning, setShowLavarWarning] = useState(false);
  const [showBloqueo, setShowBloqueo] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        Promise.all([getCliente(id), listByCliente(id)]).then(([datosCliente, listaVehiculos]) => {
          if (!active) {
            return;
          }
          setCliente(datosCliente);
          setVehiculos(listaVehiculos);
          setChecking(false);
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [id, navigate]);

  async function handleConfirmDelete() {
    try {
      await deleteCliente(id);
      navigate('/clientes');
    } catch (error) {
      setShowDelete(false);
      if (error.status === 409) {
        setShowBloqueo(true);
        return;
      }
      navigate('/clientes');
    }
  }

  async function handleConfirmDeleteVehiculo() {
    try {
      await deleteVehiculo(showDeleteVehiculo?.codigoVehi);
      const lista = await listByCliente(id);
      setVehiculos(lista);
      setShowDeleteVehiculo(null);
      navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`, { replace: true });
    } catch (error) {
      setShowDeleteVehiculo(null);
      if (error.status === 409) {
        setShowBloqueo(true);
        return;
      }
      navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`, { replace: true });
    }
  }

  function handleCancelDeleteVehiculo() {
    setShowDeleteVehiculo(null);
    navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`, { replace: true });
  }

  async function handleEditarCliente() {
    if (await tieneLavadoActivo(vehiculos.map((vehiculo) => vehiculo.codigoVehi))) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    navigate(`/clientes/${id}/editar`);
  }

  async function handleEliminarCliente() {
    if (await tieneLavadoActivo(vehiculos.map((vehiculo) => vehiculo.codigoVehi))) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    setShowDelete(true);
  }

  async function handleEditarVehiculo(vehiculo) {
    if (await tieneLavadoActivo([vehiculo.codigoVehi])) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    navigate(`/clientes/${id}/vehiculos/${vehiculo.codigoVehi}/editar`);
  }

  async function handleEliminarVehiculo(vehiculo) {
    if (await tieneLavadoActivo([vehiculo.codigoVehi])) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    setShowDeleteVehiculo(vehiculo);
  }

  async function handleLavar(vehiculo) {
    const bloqueado = await tieneLavadoActivo([vehiculo.codigoVehi]);

    if (bloqueado) {
      setShowLavarWarning(true);
      return;
    }

    navigate(`/clientes/${id}/vehiculos/${vehiculo.codigoVehi}/lavar`);
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <ClienteVehiculoInfo
        cliente={cliente}
        vehiculos={vehiculos}
        onNuevoVehiculo={() => navigate(`/clientes/${id}/vehiculos/nuevo`)}
        onEditarCliente={handleEditarCliente}
        onEliminarCliente={handleEliminarCliente}
        onEditarVehiculo={handleEditarVehiculo}
        onEliminarVehiculo={handleEliminarVehiculo}
        onLavar={handleLavar}
        onVolver={() => navigate(origen)}
      />

      {showDelete ? (
        <ConfirmPopup
          message={`¿ Desea eliminar al cliente "${cliente?.nombre ?? ''} ${cliente?.apellido ?? ''}" ?`}
          onConfirm={handleConfirmDelete}
          onCancel={() => setShowDelete(false)}
        />
      ) : null}

      {showDeleteVehiculo ? (
        <ConfirmPopup
          message={`¿ Desea eliminar el vehiculo "${showDeleteVehiculo.patenteVehi ?? ''}" ?`}
          onConfirm={handleConfirmDeleteVehiculo}
          onCancel={handleCancelDeleteVehiculo}
        />
      ) : null}

      {showLavarWarning ? (
        <Popup
          message="El vehiculo ya tiene un lavado en curso."
          onClose={() => setShowLavarWarning(false)}
        />
      ) : null}

      {showBloqueo ? (
        <Popup message={MENSAJE_LAVADO_ACTIVO} onClose={() => setShowBloqueo(false)} />
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/ClienteInfoPage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/ClienteInfoPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import ClienteInfoPage from './ClienteInfoPage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let lavadosVehiculo = [];
let deleteStatus = 204;

const cliente = {
  codigoPersona: 5,
  dni: 28853432,
  nombre: 'Juan',
  apellido: 'Perez',
  celular: 5493516699031,
  email: 'juan@gmail.com',
};

let vehiculos = [];

function baseVehiculos() {
  return [
    {
      codigoVehi: 9,
      patenteVehi: 'AC-123-DF',
      modelo: {
        codigoModeloVehi: 100,
        modeloVehi: 'Fuego',
        marca: {
          codigoMarcaVehi: 10,
          marcaVehi: 'Renault',
          categoria: { codigoCategoriaVehi: 1, categoriaVehi: 'Sedan' },
        },
      },
    },
  ];
}

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/clientes/5' && options.method === 'DELETE') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: deleteStatus < 400, status: deleteStatus });
  }
  if (url === '/api/clientes/5') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: cliente }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos/9' && options.method === 'DELETE') {
    if (deleteStatus < 400) {
      vehiculos = vehiculos.filter((vehiculo) => vehiculo.codigoVehi !== 9);
    }
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: deleteStatus < 400, status: deleteStatus });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos?cliente=5') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: vehiculos }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/lavados?vehi=9') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: lavadosVehiculo }) });
  }
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [] }) });
}

function renderPage(entry = '/clientes/5/info') {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[entry]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/info" element={<ClienteInfoPage />} />
        <Route path="/dashboard" element={<div>DASHBOARD PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/editar" element={<div>EDITAR CLIENTE PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/nuevo" element={<div>NUEVO VEHICULO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route
          path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/:vehiculoId/editar"
          element={<div>EDITAR VEHICULO PAGE</div>}
        />
        <Route
          path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/:vehiculoId/lavar"
          element={<div>NUEVO LAVADO PAGE</div>}
        />
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<div>CLIENTES PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  vehiculos = baseVehiculos();
  lavadosVehiculo = [];
  deleteStatus = 204;
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('ClienteInfoPage', () => {
  test('muestra el encabezado y la card del cliente', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LavAutos oPreto')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('28853432')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Juan')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Perez')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('juan@gmail.com')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '+ VEHICULO' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el botón Eliminar abre el popup y ACEPTAR elimina y navega a Clientes', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(clienteCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Eliminar' }));

    expect(
      screen.getByText('¿ Desea eliminar al cliente "Juan Perez" ?'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes/5',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'DELETE' }),
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTES PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('CANCELAR cierra el popup sin eliminar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(clienteCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Eliminar' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'CANCELAR' }));

    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes/5',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'DELETE' }),
    );
  });

  test('Eliminar de la card de vehículo abre el popup y ACEPTAR elimina y actualiza la lista', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const vehiculoCard = await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');
    await user.click(within(vehiculoCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Eliminar' }));

    expect(
      screen.getByText('¿ Desea eliminar el vehiculo "AC-123-DF" ?'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos/9',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'DELETE' }),
    );
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByTestId('vehiculo-card')).not.toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  test('CANCELAR cierra el popup de vehículo sin eliminar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const vehiculoCard = await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');
    await user.click(within(vehiculoCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Eliminar' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'CANCELAR' }));

    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos/9',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'DELETE' }),
    );
  });

  test('el botón + VEHICULO navega a la página Agregar Vehiculo', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(clienteCard).getByRole('button', { name: '+ VEHICULO' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('NUEVO VEHICULO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('LAVAR navega a Nuevo Lavado si el vehículo no tiene lavado en curso', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const vehiculoCard = await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');
    await user.click(within(vehiculoCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'LAVAR' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('NUEVO LAVADO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('LAVAR avisa si el vehículo ya tiene un lavado en curso', async () => {
    lavadosVehiculo = [{ codigoLavado: 1, datosEstado: { descripcionEstado: 'Lavando' } }];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const vehiculoCard = await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');
    await user.click(within(vehiculoCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'LAVAR' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('El vehiculo ya tiene un lavado en curso.'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('NUEVO LAVADO PAGE')).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));
    expect(
      screen.queryByText('El vehiculo ya tiene un lavado en curso.'),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el botón Editar de la card de vehículo navega a la edición', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const vehiculoCard = await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');
    await user.click(within(vehiculoCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Editar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('EDITAR VEHICULO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('vuelve a solicitar los vehículos al montarse', async () => {
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/vehiculos?cliente=5', {
      credentials: 'include',
    });
  });

  test('el botón Editar de la card del cliente navega a la edición', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(clienteCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Editar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('EDITAR CLIENTE PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('muestra una card por vehículo con categoría, marca, modelo y patente', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Sedan')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Renault')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Fuego')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('AC-123-DF')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'LAVAR' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el hover adelanta la card señalada y retrasa las demás', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    const vehiculoCard = await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');

    await user.hover(clienteCard);

    expect(clienteCard.className).toContain('scale-105');
    expect(vehiculoCard.className).toContain('scale-95');
  });

  test('Volver navega a Clientes', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('LavAutos oPreto');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '←' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTES PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Volver regresa al origen cuando se abrio desde otra pantalla', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage({
      pathname: '/clientes/5/info',
      state: { from: '/dashboard?modo=clientes&fecha=2026-05-01' },
    });
    await screen.findByText('LavAutos oPreto');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '←' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('DASHBOARD PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Editar cliente con lavado activo muestra el aviso y no navega', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavadosVehiculo = [{ datosEstado: { descripcionEstado: 'En Espera' } }];
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(clienteCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Editar' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('Edicion / Eliminacion cancelada...Vehiculo en estado de Lavado'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('EDITAR CLIENTE PAGE')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Eliminar cliente con lavado activo muestra el aviso y no abre la confirmación', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavadosVehiculo = [{ datosEstado: { descripcionEstado: 'Lavando' } }];
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(clienteCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Eliminar' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('Edicion / Eliminacion cancelada...Vehiculo en estado de Lavado'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Editar vehiculo con lavado activo muestra el aviso y no navega', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavadosVehiculo = [{ datosEstado: { descripcionEstado: 'Terminado' } }];
    renderPage();

    const vehiculoCard = await screen.findByTestId('vehiculo-card');
    await user.click(within(vehiculoCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Editar' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('Edicion / Eliminacion cancelada...Vehiculo en estado de Lavado'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('EDITAR VEHICULO PAGE')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Eliminar vehiculo con lavado activo muestra el aviso y no abre la confirmación', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavadosVehiculo = [{ datosEstado: { descripcionEstado: 'En Espera' } }];
    renderPage();

    await user.click(within(vehiculoCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Eliminar' }));

      await screen.findByText('Edicion / Eliminacion cancelada...Vehiculo en estado de Lavado'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('ACEPTAR con 409 muestra el aviso y no navega', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    deleteStatus = 409;
    renderPage();

    const clienteCard = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(clienteCard).getByRole('button', { name: 'Eliminar' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('Edicion / Eliminacion cancelada...Vehiculo en estado de Lavado'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('CLIENTES PAGE')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
