===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/ClientesPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import AppHeader from '../components/AppHeader.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { searchClientes } from '../services/clientService.js';

const COLUMNS = ['DNI', 'NOMBRE', 'APELLIDO', 'CELULAR'];
const ROW_COLORS = ['#f4e6e6', '#b4b2b2'];

export default function ClientesPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [term, setTerm] = useState('');
  const [results, setResults] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        } else {
          setChecking(false);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [navigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const trimmed = term.trim();
    if (trimmed.length < 4) {
      setResults([]);
      return undefined;
    }

    const id = setTimeout(() => {
      searchClientes(trimmed)
        .then(setResults)
        .catch(() => setResults([]));
    }, 300);

    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [term]);

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <AppHeader />

      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border-4 border-gray-500 p-6">
        <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-[#2637a2] px-6 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-white">
          CLIENTES
        </span>

        <div className="mx-auto mt-4 flex max-w-2xl items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl bg-[#8f8a8a] p-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={term}
            onChange={(event) => setTerm(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Dni, Nombre, Apellido..."
            aria-label="Buscar clientes"
            className="flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#f4e6e6] px-4 py-2 text-brand-navy outline-none"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate('/clientes/nuevo')}
            className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-6 py-2 font-bold text-white"
          >
            + CLIENTE
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-2xl rounded-2xl bg-[#2637a2] p-4">
          <table className="w-full text-center">
            <thead>
              <tr className="text-gray-400">
                {COLUMNS.map((col) => (
                  <th key={col} className="p-2 text-sm font-bold">
                    {col}
                  </th>
                ))}
                <th className="p-2" />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {results.length > 0 ? (
                results.map((cliente, index) => (
                  <tr
                    key={cliente.codigoPersona}
                    data-testid="cliente-row"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: ROW_COLORS[index % ROW_COLORS.length] }}
                  >
                    <td className="p-2">{cliente.dni}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">{cliente.nombre}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">{cliente.apellido}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">{cliente.celular}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigate(`/clientes/${cliente.codigoPersona}/info`)}
                        className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-4 py-1 font-bold text-white"
                      >
                        + info
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              ) : (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={COLUMNS.length + 1} className="p-6 text-center font-bold text-gray-400">
                    Busqueda sin resultados ...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate('/home')}
            className="rounded-2xl bg-green-600 px-4 py-2 text-xl font-bold text-white"
          >
            ← 
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/ClientesPage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/ClientesPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import ClientesPage from './ClientesPage.jsx';

let searchResults = [];
let sessionOk = true;

function mockFetch(url) {
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/clientes?buscar=')) {
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: true,
      status: 200,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ success: true, data: searchResults }),
    });
  }
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, status: 204 });
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/clientes']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<ClientesPage />} />
        <Route path="/clientes/nuevo" element={<div>NUEVO CLIENTE PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/info" element={<div>CLIENTE INFO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/home" element={<div>HOME PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  searchResults = [];
  sessionOk = true;
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('ClientesPage', () => {
  test('muestra la etiqueta CLIENTES, el buscador, + CLIENTE y Volver', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('CLIENTES')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '+ CLIENTE' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '←' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('con menos de 4 caracteres no se consulta', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar clientes'), 'jua');

    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.stringContaining('/api/clientes?buscar='),
      expect.anything(),
    );
  });

  test('con 4 o más caracteres consulta y muestra resultados', async () => {
    searchResults = [
      { codigoPersona: 1, dni: 28853432, nombre: 'JUAN', apellido: 'alvarez', celular: 28853432 },
    ];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar clientes'), 'juan');

    expect(await screen.findByText('alvarez')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin coincidencias muestra "Busqueda sin resultados ..."', async () => {
    searchResults = [];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar clientes'), 'zzzz');

    expect(await screen.findByText('Busqueda sin resultados ...')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('muestra filas alternadas y un botón + info por fila', async () => {
    searchResults = [
      { codigoPersona: 1, dni: 111, nombre: 'A', apellido: 'B', celular: 1 },
      { codigoPersona: 2, dni: 222, nombre: 'C', apellido: 'D', celular: 2 },
    ];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar clientes'), 'abcd');

    const rows = await screen.findAllByTestId('cliente-row');
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(rows[0].style.backgroundColor).toContain('244');
    expect(rows[1].style.backgroundColor).toContain('180');
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: '+ info' })).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  test('el botón + info navega a la información del cliente', async () => {
    searchResults = [
      { codigoPersona: 5, dni: 28853432, nombre: 'JUAN', apellido: 'alvarez', celular: 28853432 },
    ];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar clientes'), 'juan');
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: '+ info' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTE INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el botón + CLIENTE navega a Nuevo Cliente', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '+ CLIENTE' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('NUEVO CLIENTE PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Volver navega al home', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '←' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('HOME PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el ícono de usuario abre el popup y CERRAR SESION navega al login', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /usuario/i }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'CERRAR SESION' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/auth/logout',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('la X cierra el popup sin cerrar sesión', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar clientes');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /usuario/i }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'CERRAR SESION' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/ClientesPage.test.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import AppHeader from '../components/AppHeader.jsx';
import LavadoInfoPopup from '../components/LavadoInfoPopup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { listClientesSinLavados } from '../services/clientService.js';
import { listEstadosLavado, listLavados } from '../services/lavadoService.js';

function normalizar(texto) {
  return String(texto ?? '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

function fechaLocalHoy() {
  const hoy = new Date();
  const mes = String(hoy.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const dia = String(hoy.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${hoy.getFullYear()}-${mes}-${dia}`;
}

const HOY = fechaLocalHoy();

const campoBase = 'w-32 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-sm text-brand-navy outline-none';

function Campo({ etiqueta, valor }) {
  return (
    <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">{etiqueta}</span>
      <input
        readOnly
        aria-label={etiqueta}
        value={valor ?? ''}
        className={`${campoBase} bg-brand-pink`}
      />
    </label>
  );
}

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [lavados, setLavados] = useState([]);
  const [clientes, setClientes] = useState([]);
  const [infoLavado, setInfoLavado] = useState(null);

  const modo = searchParams.get('modo');
  const desde = searchParams.get('desde') ?? '';
  const hasta = searchParams.get('hasta') ?? '';
  const fecha = searchParams.get('fecha') ?? '';

  const habilitadoLavados = modo === 'lavados';
  const habilitadoClientes = modo === 'clientes';

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        setChecking(false);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [navigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (checking) {
      return undefined;
    }

    let active = true;

    async function cargar() {
      if (modo === 'lavados' && desde && hasta) {
        const estados = await listEstadosLavado();
        const entregado = estados.find(
          (estado) => normalizar(estado.descripcionEstado) === 'entregado',
        );
        const lista = entregado
          ? await listLavados({ estado: entregado.codigoEstado, desde, hasta })
          : [];
        if (active) {
          setLavados(lista);
          setClientes([]);
        }
      } else if (modo === 'clientes' && fecha) {
        const lista = await listClientesSinLavados(fecha);
        if (active) {
          setClientes(lista);
          setLavados([]);
        }
      }
    }

    cargar();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [checking, modo, desde, hasta, fecha]);

  function seleccionarModo(nuevoModo) {
    setLavados([]);
    setClientes([]);
    setInfoLavado(null);
    setSearchParams({ modo: nuevoModo });
  }

  function cambiarFecha(campo, valor) {
    const siguiente = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
    if (valor) {
      siguiente.set(campo, valor);
    } else {
      siguiente.delete(campo);
    }
    siguiente.set('modo', campo === 'fecha' ? 'clientes' : 'lavados');
    setSearchParams(siguiente);
  }

  function abrirCliente(cliente) {
    navigate(`/clientes/${cliente.codigoPersona}/info`, {
      state: { from: `/dashboard?${searchParams.toString()}` },
    });
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <AppHeader />

      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-5xl rounded-3xl border-3 border-blue-900 p-6">
        <span className="inline-block rounded-2xl bg-brand-navy px-8 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white">
          DASHBOARD
        </span>

        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row">
          <aside className="w-full max-w-[220px] rounded-3xl bg-[#eed8d8] p-4">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-8">
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={habilitadoLavados}
                onClick={() => seleccionarModo('lavados')}
                className={`rounded-lg py-2 text-sm font-bold text-brand-navy hover:bg-[#7eb93c] ${
                  habilitadoLavados ? 'bg-[#7eb93c]' : 'bg-[#eed8d8]'
                }`}
              >
                REGISTRO LAVADOS
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={habilitadoClientes}
                onClick={() => seleccionarModo('clientes')}
                className={`rounded-lg py-2 text-sm font-bold text-brand-navy hover:bg-[#7eb93c] ${
                  habilitadoClientes ? 'bg-[#7eb93c]' : 'bg-[#eed8d8]'
                }`}
              >
                REGISTRO CLIENTES
              </button>
            </div>
          </aside>

          <div className="flex-1 space-y-6">
            <fieldset disabled={!habilitadoLavados}>
              <div
                className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-full px-5 py-2 ${
                  habilitadoLavados ? 'bg-[#7eb93c]' : 'bg-[#8f8a8a]'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Registro de Lavados...</span>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Desde...</span>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    aria-label="Desde..."
                    value={desde}
                    max={HOY}
                    onChange={(event) => cambiarFecha('desde', event.target.value)}
                    className={`${campoBase} ${
                      habilitadoLavados ? 'bg-brand-pink' : 'bg-[#8f8a8a]'
                    }`}
                  />
                </label>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Hasta...</span>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    aria-label="Hasta..."
                    value={hasta}
                    min={desde || undefined}
                    max={HOY}
                    onChange={(event) => cambiarFecha('hasta', event.target.value)}
                    className={`${campoBase} ${
                      habilitadoLavados ? 'bg-brand-pink' : 'bg-[#8f8a8a]'
                    }`}
                  />
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
                {lavados.map((lavado) => (
                  <div
                    key={lavado.codigoLavado}
                    className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl p-4 ${
                      habilitadoLavados ? 'bg-[#2637a2]' : 'bg-[#8f8a8a]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Campo etiqueta="N° Lavado" valor={lavado.codigoLavado} />
                    <Campo etiqueta="Categoria" valor={lavado.categoria?.descripcionLavado} />
                    <Campo etiqueta="Patente" valor={lavado.vehiculo?.patenteVehi} />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setInfoLavado(lavado)}
                      className="ml-auto rounded-full bg-brand-green px-4 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                    >
                      + info
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>

            <fieldset disabled={!habilitadoClientes}>
              <div
                className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-full px-5 py-2 ${
                  habilitadoClientes ? 'bg-[#cc0000]' : 'bg-[#8f8a8a]'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                  Clientes que NO vuelven desde...
                </span>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Seleccione...</span>
                  <input
                    type="date"
                    aria-label="Seleccione..."
                    value={fecha}
                    max={HOY}
                    onChange={(event) => cambiarFecha('fecha', event.target.value)}
                    className={`${campoBase} ${
                      habilitadoClientes ? 'bg-brand-pink' : 'bg-[#8f8a8a]'
                    }`}
                  />
                </label>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
                {clientes.map((cliente) => (
                  <div
                    key={cliente.codigoPersona}
                    className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl p-4 ${
                      habilitadoClientes ? 'bg-[#2637a2]' : 'bg-[#8f8a8a]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <Campo etiqueta="Nombre" valor={cliente.nombre} />
                    <Campo etiqueta="Apellido" valor={cliente.apellido} />
                    <Campo etiqueta="Celular" valor={cliente.celular} />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => abrirCliente(cliente)}
                      className="ml-auto rounded-full bg-brand-green px-4 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                    >
                      + info
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate('/home')}
            className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
          >
            ← Volver
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {infoLavado ? (
        <LavadoInfoPopup lavado={infoLavado} onClose={() => setInfoLavado(null)} />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.test.jsx =====
import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import DashboardPage from './DashboardPage.jsx';

const ESTADOS = [{ codigoEstado: 4, descripcionEstado: 'Entregado' }];

let sessionOk = true;
let lavados = [];
let clientes = [];
let lavadoQuery = null;
let clienteQuery = null;

function data(value) {
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: value }) });
}

function mockFetch(url) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: sessionOk,
      status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: sessionOk ? { codigoLogin: 1 } : null }),
    });
  }
  if (url === '/api/estados-lavado') {
    return data(ESTADOS);
  }
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/lavados')) {
    lavadoQuery = url;
    return data(lavados);
  }
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/clientes?sinLavadosDesde=')) {
    clienteQuery = url;
    return data(clientes);
  }
  return data([]);
}

function ClienteInfoStub() {
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  return (
    <div>
      <p>CLIENTE INFO STUB</p>
      <p>{location.state?.from ?? 'SIN ORIGEN'}</p>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => navigate(location.state?.from ?? '/clientes')}
      >
        Volver
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function renderPage(initial = '/dashboard') {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initial]}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/dashboard" element={<DashboardPage />} />
        <Route path="/home" element={<div>HOME PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<div>CLIENTES PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/info" element={<ClienteInfoStub />} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  lavadoQuery = null;
  clienteQuery = null;
  lavados = [
    {
      codigoLavado: 12,
      categoria: { descripcionLavado: 'Completo' },
      vehiculo: {
        patenteVehi: 'AB-123-CD',
        modelo: { modeloVehi: 'Corsa', marca: { marcaVehi: 'Chevrolet' } },
        datosCliente: {
          persona: {
            dni: 30111222,
            nombre: 'Ana',
            apellido: 'Perez',
            celular: '1155512345',
            email: 'ana@correo.com',
          },
        },
      },
    },
  ];
  clientes = [{ codigoPersona: 5, nombre: 'Ana', apellido: 'Perez', celular: '1155512345' }];
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('DashboardPage', () => {
  test('muestra la etiqueta DASHBOARD, los dos registros y el boton Volver', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('DASHBOARD')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'DASHBOARD' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Registro de Lavados...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Clientes que NO vuelven desde...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /volver/i })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin sesion valida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el boton Volver navega al home', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();

    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /volver/i }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('HOME PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('al entrar los dos registros estan deshabilitados', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Desde...')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Hasta...')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Seleccione...')).toBeDisabled();

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO LAVADOS' }).className).toContain(
      'bg-[#eed8d8]',
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO CLIENTES' }).className).toContain(
      'bg-[#eed8d8]',
    );
  });

  test('Registro Lavados habilita su registro y deshabilita el otro', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Desde...');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO LAVADOS' }));

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Desde...')).toBeEnabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Hasta...')).toBeEnabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Seleccione...')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO LAVADOS' }).className).toContain(
      'bg-[#7eb93c]',
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO CLIENTES' }).className).toContain(
      'bg-[#eed8d8]',
    );
  });

  test('Registro Clientes habilita su registro y deshabilita el de lavados', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Desde...');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO LAVADOS' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO CLIENTES' }));

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Seleccione...')).toBeEnabled();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Desde...')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO CLIENTES' }).className).toContain(
      'bg-[#7eb93c]',
    );
  });

  test('elegir Desde y Hasta consulta los lavados entregados del rango', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Desde...');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO LAVADOS' }));

    const desde = screen.getByLabelText('Desde...');
    const hasta = screen.getByLabelText('Hasta...');
    expect(desde).toHaveAttribute('max');
    expect(hasta).toHaveAttribute('max');

    fireEvent.change(desde, { target: { value: '2026-01-01' } });
    expect(hasta).toHaveAttribute('min', '2026-01-01');

    fireEvent.change(hasta, { target: { value: '2026-01-31' } });

    expect(await screen.findByDisplayValue('12')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(lavadoQuery).toBe('/api/lavados?estado=4&desde=2026-01-01&hasta=2026-01-31');
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('Completo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('AB-123-CD')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('+info del lavado abre la ventana y Aceptar la cierra conservando resultados', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage('/dashboard?modo=lavados&desde=2026-01-01&hasta=2026-01-31');
    await screen.findByDisplayValue('12');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '+ info' }));

    const dialogo = screen.getByRole('dialog');
    expect(within(dialogo).getByText('Chevrolet')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialogo).getByText('ana@correo.com')).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(within(dialogo).getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('12')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('elegir la fecha de clientes consulta los que no vuelven', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Seleccione...');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO CLIENTES' }));

    const fecha = screen.getByLabelText('Seleccione...');
    expect(fecha).toHaveAttribute('max');

    fireEvent.change(fecha, { target: { value: '2026-05-01' } });

    expect(await screen.findByDisplayValue('Ana')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(clienteQuery).toBe('/api/clientes?sinLavadosDesde=2026-05-01');
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('Perez')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('1155512345')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('+info del cliente abre su informacion y Volver restaura la busqueda', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Seleccione...');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'REGISTRO CLIENTES' }));
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Seleccione...'), {
      target: { value: '2026-05-01' },
    });
    await screen.findByDisplayValue('Ana');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '+ info' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTE INFO STUB')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('/dashboard?modo=clientes&fecha=2026-05-01')).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Volver' }));

    expect(await screen.findByDisplayValue('Ana')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(clienteQuery).toBe('/api/clientes?sinLavadosDesde=2026-05-01');
  });
});
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/DashboardPage.test.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarClientePage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import userIcon from '../assets/usuario.png';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { getCliente, searchClientes, updateCliente } from '../services/clientService.js';
import { isValidEmail, onlyDigits, onlyLetters } from '../utils/validators.js';

const EMPTY_FORM = { dni: '', nombre: '', apellido: '', celular: '', email: '' };

function toForm(cliente) {
  return {
    dni: cliente?.dni != null ? String(cliente.dni) : '',
    nombre: cliente?.nombre || '',
    apellido: cliente?.apellido || '',
    celular: cliente?.celular != null ? String(cliente.celular) : '',
    email: cliente?.email || '',
  };
}

export default function EditarClientePage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [original, setOriginal] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [warning, setWarning] = useState('');
  const [popup, setPopup] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        getCliente(id).then((cliente) => {
          if (!active) {
            return;
          }
          const datos = toForm(cliente);
          setOriginal(datos);
          setForm(datos);
          setChecking(false);
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [id, navigate]);

  function updateField(name, value) {
    setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, [name]: value }));
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setWarning('');

    const incompleto = Object.values(form).some((value) => !String(value).trim());
    if (incompleto) {
      setWarning('Todos los campos son obligatorios.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidEmail(form.email)) {
      setWarning('El e-mail no tiene un formato válido.');
      return;
    }

    const dni = String(form.dni).trim();
    const existentes = await searchClientes(dni);
    const duplicado = existentes.some(
      (cliente) => String(cliente.dni) === dni && String(cliente.codigoPersona) !== String(id),
    );

    if (duplicado) {
      setPopup({ message: 'Ese cliente ya se encuentra registrado.', action: 'reset' });
      return;
    }

    try {
      await updateCliente(id, {
        nombre: form.nombre,
        apellido: form.apellido,
        dni: form.dni,
        email: form.email,
        celular: form.celular,
      });
      setPopup({ message: 'La edición de datos se efectuó satisfactoriamente.', action: 'info' });
    } catch {
      setWarning('No se pudo guardar la edición.');
    }
  }

  function handlePopupClose() {
    const action = popup?.action;
    setPopup(null);
    if (action === 'reset') {
      setForm(original);
    } else if (action === 'info') {
      navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`);
    }
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  const inputClass = 'mt-1 w-full rounded-full bg-brand-input px-4 py-2 text-white outline-none';
  const labelClass = 'block text-sm font-bold text-white';

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl bg-brand-pink p-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#7eb93c]">Editar Cliente</h1>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-brand-indigo p-4">
            <div className="mb-3 flex justify-center">
              <img src={userIcon} alt="Usuario" className="h-12 w-12" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="dni" className={labelClass}>
                  DNI
                </label>
                <input
                  id="dni"
                  name="dni"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.dni}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('dni', onlyDigits(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="nombre" className={labelClass}>
                  Nombre
                </label>
                <input
                  id="nombre"
                  name="nombre"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.nombre}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('nombre', onlyLetters(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="apellido" className={labelClass}>
                  Apellido
                </label>
                <input
                  id="apellido"
                  name="apellido"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.apellido}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('apellido', onlyLetters(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="celular" className={labelClass}>
                  Celular
                </label>
                <input
                  id="celular"
                  name="celular"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.celular}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('celular', onlyDigits(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="email" className={labelClass}>
                  e-m@il
                </label>
                <input
                  id="email"
                  name="email"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.email}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('email', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {warning ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
              {warning}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-6">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-full bg-[#7eb93c] px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Registrar
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`)}
              className="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {popup ? <Popup message={popup.message} onClose={handlePopupClose} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarClientePage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarClientePage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import EditarClientePage from './EditarClientePage.jsx';
import ClienteInfoPage from './ClienteInfoPage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let searchDuplicado = [];
let clienteActual = {
  codigoPersona: 5,
  dni: 28853432,
  nombre: 'Juan',
  apellido: 'Perez',
  celular: 555,
  email: 'juan@gmail.com',
};

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/clientes/5' && options.method === 'PUT') {
    clienteActual = { ...clienteActual, ...JSON.parse(options.body) };
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: clienteActual }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/clientes/5') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: clienteActual }) });
  }
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/clientes?buscar=')) {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: searchDuplicado }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos?cliente=5') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [] }) });
  }
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [] }) });
}

function renderPage({ realInfo = false } = {}) {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/clientes/5/editar']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/editar" element={<EditarClientePage />} />
        <Route
          path="/clientes/:id/info"
          element={realInfo ? <ClienteInfoPage /> : <div>INFO PAGE</div>}
        />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  searchDuplicado = [];
  clienteActual = {
    codigoPersona: 5,
    dni: 28853432,
    nombre: 'Juan',
    apellido: 'Perez',
    celular: 555,
    email: 'juan@gmail.com',
  };
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('EditarClientePage', () => {
  test('precarga los datos del cliente en el formulario', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('Editar Cliente')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('DNI')).toHaveValue('28853432');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre')).toHaveValue('Juan');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Apellido')).toHaveValue('Perez');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Celular')).toHaveValue('555');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('e-m@il')).toHaveValue('juan@gmail.com');
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('mantiene los tipos de datos (solo números)', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Cliente');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('DNI'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('DNI'), '12a3');

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('DNI')).toHaveValue('123');
  });

  test('con e-mail inválido advierte y no guarda', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Cliente');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('e-m@il'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('e-m@il'), 'juan@');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('El e-mail no tiene un formato válido.');
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes/5',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
  });

  test('Registrar guarda, muestra el popup y vuelve a la información', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Cliente');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'), 'Editado');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('La edición de datos se efectuó satisfactoriamente.'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Cancelar vuelve a la información sin guardar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Cliente');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes/5',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
  });

  test('con el dni de otro cliente muestra el popup y restablece los datos originales', async () => {
    searchDuplicado = [{ codigoPersona: 99, dni: 28853432 }];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Cliente');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'), 'Otro');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Ese cliente ya se encuentra registrado.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre')).toHaveValue('Juan');
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes/5',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
  });

  test('tras editar y volver, la información muestra los datos actualizados', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage({ realInfo: true });
    await screen.findByText('Editar Cliente');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'), 'Editado');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));
    await screen.findByText('La edición de datos se efectuó satisfactoriamente.');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Editado')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarLavadoPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import BrandHeader from '../components/BrandHeader.jsx';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import {
  getLavado,
  listCategoriasLavado,
  listEstadosLavado,
  listUtileria,
  updateLavado,
  updateUtileria,
} from '../services/lavadoService.js';

function codigo(valor) {
  return String(valor);
}

export default function EditarLavadoPage() {
  const { lavadoId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [lavado, setLavado] = useState(null);
  const [utilerias, setUtilerias] = useState([]);
  const [categorias, setCategorias] = useState([]);
  const [estados, setEstados] = useState([]);
  const [seleccion, setSeleccion] = useState([]);
  const [categoria, setCategoria] = useState('');
  const [estado, setEstado] = useState('');
  const [detalles, setDetalles] = useState('');
  const [warning, setWarning] = useState('');
  const [showSuccess, setShowSuccess] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }

        Promise.all([
          getLavado(lavadoId),
          listUtileria(),
          listCategoriasLavado(),
          listEstadosLavado(),
        ])
          .then(([datosLavado, listaUtileria, listaCategorias, listaEstados]) => {
            if (!active) {
              return;
            }
            if (!datosLavado) {
              navigate('/lavados', { replace: true });
              return;
            }
            setLavado(datosLavado);
            setUtilerias(listaUtileria);
            setCategorias(listaCategorias);
            setEstados(listaEstados);
            setSeleccion(
              listaUtileria
                .filter((utileria) => Boolean(utileria.estadoUtileria))
                .map((utileria) => codigo(utileria.codigoUtileria)),
            );
            setCategoria(codigo(datosLavado.categoriaLavado));
            setEstado(codigo(datosLavado.estado));
            setDetalles(datosLavado.detallesLavado ?? '');
            setChecking(false);
          })
          .catch(() => {
            if (active) {
              navigate('/lavados', { replace: true });
            }
          });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [lavadoId, navigate]);

  async function toggleUtileria(codigoUtileria) {
    const activa = seleccion.includes(codigoUtileria);
    const nuevoEstado = !activa;

    try {
      await updateUtileria(codigoUtileria, { estadoUtileria: nuevoEstado });
      setSeleccion((prev) =>
        nuevoEstado ? [...prev, codigoUtileria] : prev.filter((item) => item !== codigoUtileria),
      );
      setWarning('');
    } catch {
      setWarning('No se pudo actualizar la utileria.');
    }
  }

  async function handleRegistrar() {
    try {
      await updateLavado(lavadoId, {
        categoriaLavado: categoria,
        estado,
        utileria: seleccion[0],
        detallesLavado: detalles,
      });
      setShowSuccess(true);
    } catch {
      setWarning('No se pudo editar el lavado.');
    }
  }

  function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setWarning('');

    if (!categoria) {
      setWarning('Seleccione una categoria.');
      return;
    }
    if (!estado) {
      setWarning('Seleccione un estado.');
      return;
    }
    if (seleccion.length === 0) {
      setWarning('Seleccione al menos una utileria.');
      return;
    }

    handleRegistrar();
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  const labelClass = 'block text-sm font-bold text-white';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <BrandHeader />

      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border-3 border-blue-900 bg-[#8f8a8a] p-6">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-navy">
          Editar Lavado: <span className="font-extrabold">{lavado?.vehiculo?.patenteVehi}</span>
        </h1>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-[#2637a2] p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-8">
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
              {utilerias.map((utileria) => {
                const valor = codigo(utileria.codigoUtileria);
                return (
                  <label
                    key={valor}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                  >
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={seleccion.includes(valor)}
                      onChange={() => toggleUtileria(valor)}
                      className="h-5 w-5"
                    />
                    {utileria.descripcionUtileria}
                  </label>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
              {categorias.map((item) => {
                const valor = codigo(item.codigoCategoriaLavado);
                const activa = categoria === valor;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={valor}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                  >
                    <span>{item.descripcionLavado}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="switch"
                      aria-checked={activa}
                      aria-label={item.descripcionLavado}
                      onClick={() => setCategoria(valor)}
                      className={`h-6 w-12 rounded-full p-1 transition-colors ${
                        activa ? 'bg-green-600' : 'bg-red-600'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span
                        className={`block h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform ${
                          activa ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-0'
                        }`}
                      />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6">
            <label htmlFor="detalles" className={labelClass}>
              Detalles
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="detalles"
              value={detalles}
              onChange={(event) => setDetalles(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Detalles..."
              rows={3}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-2xl bg-brand-input px-4 py-2 text-white outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-white px-4 py-2">
              <label htmlFor="estado" className={labelClass}>
                Estado...
              </label>
              <select
                id="estado"
                value={estado}
                onChange={(event) => setEstado(event.target.value)}
                className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1 text-brand-navy outline-none"
              >
                <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                {estados.map((item) => (
                  <option key={item.codigoEstado} value={item.codigoEstado}>
                    {item.descripcionEstado}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {warning ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-center text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
              {warning}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-6">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Registrar
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate('/lavados')}
              className="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {showSuccess ? (
        <Popup message="Lavado editado exitosamente" onClose={() => navigate('/lavados')} />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarLavadoPage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarLavadoPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import EditarLavadoPage from './EditarLavadoPage.jsx';

const ESTADOS = [
  { codigoEstado: 1, descripcionEstado: 'En Espera' },
  { codigoEstado: 2, descripcionEstado: 'Lavando' },
  { codigoEstado: 3, descripcionEstado: 'Terminado' },
  { codigoEstado: 4, descripcionEstado: 'Entregado' },
];

let sessionOk = true;
let lavado = null;
let utilerias = [];
let categorias = [];
let putLavadoBody = null;
let putLavadoOk = true;

function data(value) {
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: value }) });
}

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: sessionOk,
      status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: sessionOk ? { codigoLogin: 1 } : null }),
    });
  }
  if (url === '/api/lavados/7' && options.method === 'PUT') {
    putLavadoBody = JSON.parse(options.body);
    return putLavadoOk
      ? data({ codigoLavado: 7 })
      : Promise.resolve({ ok: false, status: 400, json: () => Promise.resolve({}) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/lavados/7') {
    return lavado
      ? data(lavado)
      : Promise.resolve({ ok: false, status: 404, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: null }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/utilerias') {
    return data(utilerias);
  }
  if (url === '/api/categorias-lavado') {
    return data(categorias);
  }
  if (url === '/api/estados-lavado') {
    return data(ESTADOS);
  }
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/utilerias/')) {
    return data({ estadoUtileria: true });
  }
  return data([]);
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/lavados/7/editar']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/lavados/:lavadoId/editar" element={<EditarLavadoPage />} />
        <Route path="/lavados" element={<div>LAVADOS PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  putLavadoBody = null;
  putLavadoOk = true;
  lavado = {
    codigoLavado: 7,
    categoriaLavado: 10,
    estado: 1,
    detallesLavado: 'Lavado inicial',
    vehiculo: { patenteVehi: 'AC-125-BF' },
  };
  utilerias = [
    { codigoUtileria: 1, descripcionUtileria: 'Auxilio', estadoUtileria: true },
    { codigoUtileria: 2, descripcionUtileria: 'Balizas', estadoUtileria: false },
  ];
  categorias = [
    { codigoCategoriaLavado: 10, descripcionLavado: 'Basico' },
    { codigoCategoriaLavado: 11, descripcionLavado: 'Completo' },
  ];
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('EditarLavadoPage', () => {
  test('muestra el titulo con la patente y precarga los datos del lavado', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(
      await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Estado...')).toHaveValue('1');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Detalles')).toHaveValue('Lavado inicial');
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' })).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Balizas' })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'aria-checked',
      'true',
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Completo' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'aria-checked',
      'false',
    );
  });

  test('sin sesion valida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('un lavado inexistente vuelve a la pagina de Lavados', async () => {
    lavado = null;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LAVADOS PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('tildar una utileria actualiza su estado', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Balizas' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/utilerias/2',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Balizas' })).toBeChecked();
  });

  test('destildar una utileria actualiza su estado', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/utilerias/1',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' })).not.toBeChecked();
  });

  test('solo un toggle de categoria queda encendido', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Completo' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Completo' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'aria-checked',
      'true',
    );
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' })).toHaveAttribute(
      'aria-checked',
      'false',
    );
  });

  test('edita el campo Detalles', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    const detalles = screen.getByLabelText('Detalles');
    await user.clear(detalles);
    await user.type(detalles, 'Actualizado');

    expect(detalles).toHaveValue('Actualizado');
  });

  test('el combobox ofrece todos los estados y permite cambiarlo', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    const combo = screen.getByLabelText('Estado...');
    expect(combo.querySelectorAll('option')).toHaveLength(5);

    await user.selectOptions(combo, '3');

    expect(combo).toHaveValue('3');
  });

  test('Registrar guarda los cambios y muestra el popup de exito', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    const detalles = screen.getByLabelText('Detalles');
    await user.clear(detalles);
    await user.type(detalles, 'Actualizado');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Lavado editado exitosamente')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(putLavadoBody).toEqual({
      categoriaLavado: '10',
      estado: '1',
      utileria: '1',
      detallesLavado: 'Actualizado',
    });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('LAVADOS PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Registrar exige al menos una utileria', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Seleccione al menos una utileria.');
    expect(putLavadoBody).toBeNull();
  });

  test('un error al guardar permanece en la pagina sin popup de exito', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    putLavadoOk = false;
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('No se pudo editar el lavado.');
    expect(screen.queryByText('Lavado editado exitosamente')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Cancelar vuelve a la lista sin guardar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Editar Lavado: AC-125-BF' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('LAVADOS PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(putLavadoBody).toBeNull();
  });
});
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarLavadoPage.test.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarVehiculoPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import autoIcon from '../assets/auto.png';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import {
  getVehiculo,
  listCategorias,
  listMarcas,
  listModelos,
  listVehiculos,
  updateVehiculo,
} from '../services/vehicleService.js';
import { formatPatente, isValidPatente } from '../utils/validators.js';

const EMPTY_FORM = {
  categoria: '',
  marca: '',
  modelo: '',
  patente: '',
};

function toForm(vehiculo) {
  return {
    categoria: String(vehiculo?.modelo?.marca?.categoria?.codigoCategoriaVehi ?? ''),
    marca: String(vehiculo?.modelo?.marca?.codigoMarcaVehi ?? ''),
    modelo: String(vehiculo?.modelo?.codigoModeloVehi ?? ''),
    patente: vehiculo?.patenteVehi || '',
  };
}

export default function EditarVehiculoPage() {
  const { id, vehiculoId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [original, setOriginal] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [categorias, setCategorias] = useState([]);
  const [marcas, setMarcas] = useState([]);
  const [modelos, setModelos] = useState([]);
  const [warning, setWarning] = useState('');
  const [popup, setPopup] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        getVehiculo(vehiculoId).then((vehiculo) => {
          if (!active) {
            return;
          }
          const datos = toForm(vehiculo);
          setOriginal(datos);
          setForm(datos);
          setChecking(false);
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [vehiculoId, navigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    listCategorias().then(setCategorias);
    listMarcas().then(setMarcas);
    listModelos().then(setModelos);
  }, []);

  function updateField(name, value) {
    setForm((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev, [name]: value };
      if (name === 'categoria') {
        next.marca = '';
        next.modelo = '';
      }
      if (name === 'marca') {
        next.modelo = '';
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  const marcasFiltradas = marcas.filter(
    (marca) => String(marca.categoriaVehi) === String(form.categoria),
  );
  const modelosFiltrados = modelos.filter(
    (modelo) => String(modelo.marcaVehi) === String(form.marca),
  );

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setWarning('');

    const incompleto = Object.values(form).some((value) => !String(value).trim());
    if (incompleto) {
      setWarning('Todos los campos son obligatorios.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidPatente(form.patente)) {
      setWarning('La patente debe tener el formato AA-123-AA.');
      return;
    }

    const patente = form.patente.trim().toUpperCase();
    const existentes = await listVehiculos();
    const duplicado = existentes.some(
      (vehiculo) =>
        String(vehiculo.codigoVehi) !== String(vehiculoId) &&
        String(vehiculo.patenteVehi).trim().toUpperCase() === patente,
    );

    if (duplicado) {
      setPopup({ message: 'Error: vehiculo ya registrado', action: 'reset' });
      return;
    }

    try {
      await updateVehiculo(vehiculoId, {
        patenteVehi: form.patente,
        estadoVehi: true,
        cliente: id,
        modeloVehi: form.modelo,
      });
      setPopup({ message: 'El vehiculo se edito correctamente.', action: 'info' });
    } catch {
      setWarning('No se pudo guardar la edición.');
    }
  }

  function handlePopupClose() {
    const action = popup?.action;
    setPopup(null);
    if (action === 'reset') {
      setForm(original);
    } else if (action === 'info') {
      navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`);    
    }
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  const inputClass =
    'mt-1 w-full rounded-full bg-brand-input px-4 py-2 text-white outline-none';
  const labelClass = 'block text-sm font-bold text-white';

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl bg-brand-pink p-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#7eb93c]">Editar Vehiculo</h1>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-brand-indigo p-4">
            <div className="mb-3 flex justify-center">
              <img src={autoIcon} alt="Vehiculo" className="h-10 w-10" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="categoria" className={labelClass}>
                  Vehiculo
                </label>
                <select
                  id="categoria"
                  name="categoria"
                  value={form.categoria}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('categoria', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {categorias.map((categoria) => (
                    <option key={categoria.codigoCategoriaVehi} value={categoria.codigoCategoriaVehi}>
                      {categoria.categoriaVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="marca" className={labelClass}>
                  Marca
                </label>
                <select
                  id="marca"
                  name="marca"
                  value={form.marca}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('marca', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {marcasFiltradas.map((marca) => (
                    <option key={marca.codigoMarcaVehi} value={marca.codigoMarcaVehi}>
                      {marca.marcaVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="modelo" className={labelClass}>
                  Modelo
                </label>
                <select
                  id="modelo"
                  name="modelo"
                  value={form.modelo}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('modelo', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {modelosFiltrados.map((modelo) => (
                    <option key={modelo.codigoModeloVehi} value={modelo.codigoModeloVehi}>
                      {modelo.modeloVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="patente" className={labelClass}>
                  Patente
                </label>
                <input
                  id="patente"
                  name="patente"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.patente}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('patente', formatPatente(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {warning ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
              {warning}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-6">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-full bg-[#7eb93c] px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Registrar
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`)}  
              className="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {popup ? <Popup message={popup.message} onClose={handlePopupClose} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/EditarVehiculoPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import EditarVehiculoPage from './EditarVehiculoPage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let categorias = [];
let marcas = [];
let modelos = [];
let vehiculos = [];
let putOk = true;
let putBody = null;
let vehiculoActual = null;

function data(value) {
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: value }) });
}

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/categorias-vehiculo') {
    return data(categorias);
  }
  if (url === '/api/marcas-vehiculo') {
    return data(marcas);
  }
  if (url === '/api/modelos-vehiculo') {
    return data(modelos);
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos/9' && options.method === 'PUT') {
    putBody = JSON.parse(options.body);
    return Promise.resolve(
      putOk
        ? { ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: { ...vehiculoActual, ...putBody } }) }
        : { ok: false, status: 400 },
    );
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos/9') {
    return data(vehiculoActual);
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos') {
    return data(vehiculos);
  }
  return data([]);
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/clientes/5/vehiculos/9/editar']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/:vehiculoId/editar" element={<EditarVehiculoPage />} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/info" element={<div>INFO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

  categorias = [{ codigoCategoriaVehi: 1, categoriaVehi: 'Sedan' }];
  marcas = [{ codigoMarcaVehi: 10, marcaVehi: 'Renault', categoriaVehi: 1 }];
  modelos = [{ codigoModeloVehi: 100, modeloVehi: 'Fuego', marcaVehi: 10 }];
  vehiculos = [{ codigoVehi: 9, patenteVehi: 'AC-123-DF' }];
  putOk = true;
  putBody = null;
  vehiculoActual = {
    codigoVehi: 9,
    patenteVehi: 'AC-123-DF',
    cliente: 5,
    modelo: {
      codigoModeloVehi: 100,
      modeloVehi: 'Fuego',
      marca: {
        codigoMarcaVehi: 10,
        marcaVehi: 'Renault',
        categoria: { codigoCategoriaVehi: 1, categoriaVehi: 'Sedan' },

describe('EditarVehiculoPage', () => {

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

    const user = userEvent.setup();

    await screen.findByText('Editar Vehiculo');
    await screen.findByRole('option', { name: 'Renault' });

    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Fuego' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Vehiculo'), '');
    expect(screen.queryByRole('option', { name: 'Renault' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Vehiculo'), '1');
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Renault' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Marca'), '10');
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Fuego' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('con patente inválida advierte y no guarda', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Vehiculo');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'), 'ab254m');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'La patente debe tener el formato AA-123-AA.',
    );
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos/9',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
  });

  test('con patente de otro vehículo muestra el popup y restablece los datos', async () => {
    vehiculos = [
      { codigoVehi: 9, patenteVehi: 'AC-123-DF' },
      { codigoVehi: 1, patenteVehi: 'AB-123-CD' },
    ];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Vehiculo');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'), 'ab123cd');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Error: vehiculo ya registrado')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos/9',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Patente')).toHaveValue('AC-123-DF');
  });

  test('registra la edición, muestra el popup y vuelve a la información', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Vehiculo');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'), 'ab123ef');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('El vehiculo se edito correctamente.'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(putBody).toEqual({
      patenteVehi: 'AB-123-EF',
      estadoVehi: true,
      cliente: '5',
      modeloVehi: '100',
    });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('acepta la propia patente sin marcarla como duplicada', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Vehiculo');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('El vehiculo se edito correctamente.'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(putBody).toEqual({
      patenteVehi: 'AC-123-DF',
      estadoVehi: true,
      cliente: '5',
      modeloVehi: '100',
    });
  });

  test('ante un error de guardado advierte y no navega', async () => {
    putOk = false;
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Vehiculo');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('No se pudo guardar la edición.');
    expect(screen.queryByText('INFO PAGE')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Cancelar vuelve a la información sin guardar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Editar Vehiculo');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos/9',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
  });
});
