===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/HomePage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import AppHeader from '../components/AppHeader.jsx';
import DateTimePanel from '../components/DateTimePanel.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';

const ACCESS_BUTTONS = ['CLIENTES', 'LAVADOS', 'VEHICULOS', 'DASHBOARD'];

export default function HomePage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        } else {
          setChecking(false);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [navigate]);

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <AppHeader />

      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-xl rounded-3xl border-4 border-green-700 p-6">
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-6 md:flex-row">
          <div className="w-full max-w-xs rounded-3xl border-3 border-gray-400 bg-brand-pink p-6">
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-4">
              {ACCESS_BUTTONS.map((label) => (
                <button
                  key={label}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (label === 'CLIENTES') {
                      navigate('/clientes');
                    } else if (label === 'LAVADOS') {
                      navigate('/lavados');
                    } else if (label === 'VEHICULOS') {
                      navigate('/vehiculos');
                    } else if (label === 'DASHBOARD') {
                      navigate('/dashboard');
                    }
                  }}
                  className="rounded-full bg-brand-input px-6 py-2 font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#5ea014]"
                >
                  {label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <DateTimePanel />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import HomePage from './HomePage.jsx';

function renderHome() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/home']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/home" element={<HomePage />} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<div>CLIENTES PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/vehiculos" element={<div>VEHICULOS PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/lavados" element={<div>LAVADOS PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/dashboard" element={<div>DASHBOARD PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200 });
});

  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('HomePage', () => {
  test('muestra los 4 botones de acceso y la etiqueta ADMIN', async () => {

    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'CLIENTES' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'VEHICULOS' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'DASHBOARD' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('ADMIN')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('los botones de acceso tienen el hover verde #5ea014', async () => {
    renderHome();

    const boton = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'CLIENTES' });
    expect(boton.className).toContain('hover:bg-[#5ea014]');
  });

  test('el ícono de usuario abre el popup de cierre de sesión', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderHome();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /usuario/i }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'CERRAR SESION' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('CERRAR SESION cierra la sesión y navega al login', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderHome();
    await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'CLIENTES' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /usuario/i }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'CERRAR SESION' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/auth/logout',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('la X cierra el popup sin cerrar sesión', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderHome();
    await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'CLIENTES' });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /usuario/i }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'CERRAR SESION' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/auth/logout', expect.anything());
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch.mockResolvedValue({ ok: false, status: 401 });
    renderHome();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el botón CLIENTES navega a la página de Clientes', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderHome();

    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'CLIENTES' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTES PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el botón VEHICULOS navega a la página de Vehiculos', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderHome();

    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'VEHICULOS' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('VEHICULOS PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el botón LAVADOS navega a la página de Lavados', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderHome();

    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'LAVADOS' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('LAVADOS PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('el botón DASHBOARD navega a la página Dashboard', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderHome();

    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'DASHBOARD' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('DASHBOARD PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/LavadosPage.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import bubblesIcon from '../assets/burbujas.png';
import BrandHeader from '../components/BrandHeader.jsx';
import LavadoInfoPopup from '../components/LavadoInfoPopup.jsx';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { listEstadosLavado, listLavados, updateLavado } from '../services/lavadoService.js';
import { formatTime } from '../utils/formatDateTime.js';

const SECCIONES = [
  {
    id: 'en-espera',
    titulo: 'En Espera...',
    estados: ['en espera'],
    borde: 'border-red-600',
    etiqueta: 'text-red-700',
    accion: { etiqueta: 'Lavar', popup: 'Lavando Vehiculo', destino: 'Lavando' },
  },
  {
    id: 'lavando',
    titulo: 'Lavando...',
    estados: ['lavando'],
    borde: 'border-yellow-700',
    etiqueta: 'text-yellow-700',
    accion: { etiqueta: 'Completado', popup: 'Lavado Terminado', destino: 'Terminado' },
  },
  {
    id: 'terminado',
    titulo: 'Terminado...',
    estados: ['terminado'],
    borde: 'border-green-600',
    etiqueta: 'text-green-700',
    accion: { etiqueta: 'Entregar', popup: 'Vehiculo Entregado', destino: 'Entregado' },
  },
];

function normalizar(texto) {
  return String(texto ?? '')
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

function formatHora(valor) {
  if (!valor) {
    return '';
  }
  const fecha = new Date(valor);
  if (Number.isNaN(fecha.getTime())) {
    return '';
  }
  const { hour, minute, period } = formatTime(fecha);
  return `${hour}:${minute} ${period}`;
}

function ChevronIcon({ arriba }) {
  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="h-4 w-4"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="3"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
    >
      <path d={arriba ? 'M6 15l6-6 6 6' : 'M6 9l6 6 6-6'} />
    </svg>
  );
}

function HandIcon() {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" className="h-6 w-6" fill="currentColor">
      <path d="M8.5 12.5V6a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 3 0v4.5H12V5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 3 0v5.5h.5V7a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 3 0v7.5a6.5 6.5 0 0 1-6.5 6.5h-.5A6.5 6.5 0 0 1 5 14.5v-3a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 3 0v1h.5z" />
    </svg>
  );
}

export default function LavadosPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [lavados, setLavados] = useState([]);
  const [estados, setEstados] = useState([]);
  const [colapsadas, setColapsadas] = useState({});
  const [info, setInfo] = useState(null);
  const [avance, setAvance] = useState(null);

  const cargar = useCallback(async () => {
    const [listaLavados, catalogoEstados] = await Promise.all([
      listLavados(),
      listEstadosLavado(),
    ]);
    setLavados(listaLavados);
    setEstados(catalogoEstados);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        cargar()
          .then(() => {
            if (active) {
              setChecking(false);
            }
          })
          .catch(() => {
            if (active) {
              setChecking(false);
            }
          });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [cargar, navigate]);

  async function handleAcceptAvance() {
    if (!avance) {
      return;
    }
    const { lavado, seccion } = avance;
    const destino = estados.find(
      (estado) => normalizar(estado.descripcionEstado) === normalizar(seccion.accion.destino),
    );
    setAvance(null);

    if (!destino) {
      return;
    }

    await updateLavado(lavado.codigoLavado, { estado: destino.codigoEstado });
    await cargar();
  }

  function lavadosDe(seccion) {
    return lavados.filter((lavado) =>
      seccion.estados.includes(normalizar(lavado.datosEstado?.descripcionEstado)),
    );
  }

  function toggleSeccion(id) {
    setColapsadas((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <BrandHeader />

      <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => navigate('/clientes')}
          className="animate-nuevo-lavado flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-green-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white hover:[animation-play-state:paused]"
        >
          <HandIcon />
          Nuevo Lavado
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-auto mt-8 max-w-3xl space-y-8">
        {SECCIONES.map((seccion) => {
          const items = lavadosDe(seccion);
          const colapsada = Boolean(colapsadas[seccion.id]);

          return (
            <section key={seccion.id} aria-label={seccion.titulo}>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <img
                  src={bubblesIcon}
                  alt=""
                  aria-hidden="true"
                  className="animate-burbuja h-10 w-10"
                />
                <div
                  className={`flex flex-1 items-center gap-3 rounded-full border-2 ${seccion.borde} bg-brand-input px-4 py-1`}
                >
                  <span className={`text-sm font-bold ${seccion.etiqueta}`}>{seccion.titulo}</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleSeccion(seccion.id)}
                    aria-label={`${colapsada ? 'Expandir' : 'Contraer'} ${seccion.titulo}`}
                    className={`ml-auto flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full border ${seccion.borde} ${seccion.etiqueta}`}
                  >
                    <ChevronIcon arriba={colapsada} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {colapsada ? null : (
                <div className="mt-3 space-y-4">
                  {items.map((lavado) => (
                    <div
                      key={lavado.codigoLavado}
                      className="mx-auto w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl border border-white bg-[#2637a2] p-6"
                    >
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
                        <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">N° Lavado</span>
                          <input
                            readOnly
                            aria-label="N° Lavado"
                            value={lavado.codigoLavado ?? ''}
                            className="w-20 rounded-md bg-brand-pink px-2 py-1 text-sm text-brand-navy"
                          />
                        </label>
                        <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Hora</span>
                          <input
                            readOnly
                            aria-label="Hora"
                            value={formatHora(lavado.fechaHoraLavado)}
                            className="w-28 rounded-md bg-brand-pink px-2 py-1 text-sm text-brand-navy"
                          />
                        </label>
                        <label className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Patente</span>
                          <input
                            readOnly
                            aria-label="Patente"
                            value={lavado.vehiculo?.patenteVehi ?? ''}
                            className="w-28 rounded-md bg-brand-pink px-2 py-1 text-sm text-brand-navy"
                          />
                        </label>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setInfo(lavado)}
                          className="rounded-full bg-brand-green px-4 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                        >
                          + info
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => navigate(`/lavados/${lavado.codigoLavado}/editar`)}
                          className="rounded-full bg-gray-400 px-4 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                        >
                          Editar
                        </button>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-3 flex justify-center">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setAvance({ lavado, seccion })}
                          className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
                        >
                          {seccion.accion.etiqueta}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 flex justify-center">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => navigate('/home')}
          className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
        >
          ←
        </button>
      </div>

      {info ? <LavadoInfoPopup lavado={info} onClose={() => setInfo(null)} /> : null}

      {avance ? (
        <Popup message={avance.seccion.accion.popup} onClose={handleAcceptAvance} />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}




===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/LavadosPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import LavadosPage from './LavadosPage.jsx';

const ESTADOS = [
  { codigoEstado: 1, descripcionEstado: 'En Espera' },
  { codigoEstado: 2, descripcionEstado: 'Lavando' },
  { codigoEstado: 3, descripcionEstado: 'Terminado' },
  { codigoEstado: 4, descripcionEstado: 'Entregado' },
];

let sessionOk = true;
let lavados = [];

function data(value) {
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: value }) });
}

function crearLavado(codigoLavado, codigoEstado) {
  return {
    codigoLavado,
    fechaHoraLavado: '2026-09-07T13:05:00Z',
    estado: codigoEstado,
    datosEstado: ESTADOS.find((estado) => estado.codigoEstado === codigoEstado),
    categoria: { descripcionLavado: 'Completo' },
    vehiculo: {
      patenteVehi: `AB-12${codigoLavado}-CD`,
      modelo: { modeloVehi: 'Corsa', marca: { marcaVehi: 'Chevrolet' } },
      datosCliente: {
        persona: {
          dni: 30111222,
          nombre: 'Ana',
          apellido: 'Perez',
          celular: '1155512345',
          email: 'ana@correo.com',
        },
      },
    },
  };
}

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: sessionOk,
      status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: sessionOk ? { codigoLogin: 1 } : null }),
    });
  }
  if (url === '/api/lavados') {
    return data(lavados);
  }
  if (url === '/api/estados-lavado') {
    return data(ESTADOS);
  }
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/lavados/') && options.method === 'PUT') {
    const id = Number(url.split('/').pop());
    const body = JSON.parse(options.body);
    lavados = lavados.map((lavado) =>
      lavado.codigoLavado === id
        ? {
            ...lavado,
            estado: body.estado,
            datosEstado: ESTADOS.find(
              (estado) => String(estado.codigoEstado) === String(body.estado),
            ),
          }
        : lavado,
    );
    return data({ codigoLavado: id, estado: body.estado });
  }
  return data([]);
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/lavados']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/lavados" element={<LavadosPage />} />
        <Route path="/lavados/:lavadoId/editar" element={<div>EDITAR LAVADO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/home" element={<div>HOME PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<div>CLIENTES PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  lavados = [];
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('LavadosPage', () => {

    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('AB-121-CD')).toHaveAttribute('readonly');
  });

  test('Editar navega a la pagina de edicion del lavado', async () => {

    renderPage();

    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Editar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('EDITAR LAVADO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();

    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavados = [crearLavado(1, 1)];
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByDisplayValue('1');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Contraer En Espera...' }));
    expect(screen.queryByDisplayValue('1')).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Expandir En Espera...' }));
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('1')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('+info abre la ventana con los datos y Aceptar la cierra', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavados = [crearLavado(1, 1)];
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByDisplayValue('1');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '+ info' }));

    const dialogo = screen.getByRole('dialog');
    expect(within(dialogo).getByText('Chevrolet')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialogo).getByText('Corsa')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialogo).getByText('30111222')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialogo).getByText('ana@correo.com')).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(within(dialogo).getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(screen.queryByRole('dialog')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByDisplayValue('1')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Lavar mueve el lavado a la seccion Lavando', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavados = [crearLavado(1, 1)];
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByDisplayValue('1');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lavar' }));

    expect(screen.getByText('Lavando Vehiculo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    const lavando = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Lavando...' });
    expect(await within(lavando).findByDisplayValue('1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/lavados/1',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
  });

  test('Completado mueve el lavado a la seccion Terminado', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavados = [crearLavado(2, 2)];
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByDisplayValue('2');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Completado' }));

    expect(screen.getByText('Lavado Terminado')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    const terminado = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Terminado...' });
    expect(await within(terminado).findByDisplayValue('2')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Entregar marca el lavado como Entregado y deja de mostrarlo', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    lavados = [crearLavado(3, 3)];
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByDisplayValue('3');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Entregar' }));

    expect(screen.getByText('Vehiculo Entregado')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'En Espera...' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByDisplayValue('3')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/LoginPage.jsx =====
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import autoIcon from '../assets/auto.png';
import bubblesIcon from '../assets/burbujas.png';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { login } from '../services/authService.js';

const SUCCESS_MESSAGE = '!Bienvenid@!';
const ERROR_MESSAGE = 'Usuario / Contraseña incorrecta';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [usuario, setUsuario] = useState('');
  const [contrasena, setContrasena] = useState('');
  const [fieldError, setFieldError] = useState('');
  const [popupMessage, setPopupMessage] = useState('');
  const [isSuccess, setIsSuccess] = useState(false);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFieldError('');

    if (!usuario.trim() || !contrasena.trim()) {
      setFieldError('Usuario y contraseña son obligatorios.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const ok = await login(usuario, contrasena);
      setIsSuccess(ok);
      setPopupMessage(ok ? SUCCESS_MESSAGE : ERROR_MESSAGE);
    } catch {
      setPopupMessage(ERROR_MESSAGE);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-brand-indigo  p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl bg-brand-pink border-2 border-gray-400 px-10 py-12 shadow-2xl">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
          <img src={bubblesIcon} alt="" className="h-8 w-14" />
          <img src={autoIcon} alt="Auto de lavadero" className="h-14 w-18" />
        </div>

        <h1 className="mt-4 text-center text-4xl font-extrabold text-brand-title">
          LavAutos oPreto
        </h1>

        <form className="mt-10 space-y-6" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="usuario" className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-navy">
              Usuario
            </label>
            <input
              id="usuario"
              name="usuario"
              type="text"
              value={usuario}
              onChange={(event) => setUsuario(event.target.value)}
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-full bg-brand-input px-5 py-3 text-white outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="contrasena" className="block text-lg font-bold text-brand-navy">
              Contraseña
            </label>
            <input
              id="contrasena"
              name="contrasena"
              type="password"
              value={contrasena}
              onChange={(event) => setContrasena(event.target.value)}
              className="mt-2 w-full rounded-full bg-brand-input px-5 py-3 text-white outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          {fieldError ? (
            <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
              {fieldError}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          {/* <button
            type="submit"
            className=" ml-30  rounded-full bg-brand-green py-3 text-xl font-bold text-white"
          >
            Iniciar Sesion
          </button> */}

          <button
  type="submit"
  className="mx-auto block px-12 py-3 text-xl font-bold text-white rounded-full bg-brand-green"
>
  Iniciar Sesion
</button>

 
        </form>
      </div>

      {popupMessage ? (
        <Popup
          message={popupMessage}
          onClose={() => {
            setPopupMessage('');
            if (isSuccess) {
              navigate('/home');
            }
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/LoginPage.jsx =====

import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import LoginPage from './LoginPage.jsx';

function renderLogin() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/login']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
        <Route path="/home" element={<div>HOME PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn();
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('LoginPage', () => {
  test('muestra el título, los campos y el botón', () => {
    renderLogin();

    expect(screen.getByText('LavAutos oPreto')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Usuario')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Iniciar Sesion' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('no llama al backend y avisa si hay campos vacíos', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderLogin();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Iniciar Sesion' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('envía las credenciales al backend con credentials include', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch.mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200 });
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderLogin();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Usuario'), 'admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), '123456');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Iniciar Sesion' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/auth/login',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' }),
    );
  });

  test('muestra el popup de éxito con !Bienvenid@!', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch.mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200 });
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderLogin();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Usuario'), 'admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), '123456');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Iniciar Sesion' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('!Bienvenid@!')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('al cerrar el popup de éxito navega al home', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch.mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200 });
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderLogin();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Usuario'), 'admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), '123456');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Iniciar Sesion' }));
    await screen.findByText('!Bienvenid@!');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('HOME PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('muestra el popup de error cuando el login falla', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch.mockResolvedValue({ ok: false, status: 401 });
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderLogin();

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Usuario'), 'admin');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), 'mal');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Iniciar Sesion' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Usuario / Contraseña incorrecta')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
