===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoClientePage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import autoIcon from '../assets/auto.png';
import userIcon from '../assets/usuario.png';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { createCliente, searchClientes } from '../services/clientService.js';
import {
  createVehiculo,
  listCategorias,
  listMarcas,
  listModelos,
} from '../services/vehicleService.js';
import {
  formatPatente,
  isValidEmail,
  isValidPatente,
  onlyDigits,
  onlyLetters,
} from '../utils/validators.js';

const EMPTY_FORM = {
  dni: '',
  nombre: '',
  apellido: '',
  celular: '',
  email: '',
  categoria: '',
  marca: '',
  modelo: '',
  patente: '',
};

export default function NuevoClientePage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [categorias, setCategorias] = useState([]);
  const [marcas, setMarcas] = useState([]);
  const [modelos, setModelos] = useState([]);
  const [warning, setWarning] = useState('');
  const [popup, setPopup] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        } else {
          setChecking(false);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [navigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    listCategorias().then(setCategorias);
    listMarcas().then(setMarcas);
    listModelos().then(setModelos);
  }, []);

  function updateField(name, value) {
    setForm((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev, [name]: value };
      if (name === 'categoria') {
        next.marca = '';
        next.modelo = '';
      }
      if (name === 'marca') {
        next.modelo = '';
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  const marcasFiltradas = marcas.filter(
    (marca) => String(marca.categoriaVehi) === String(form.categoria),
  );
  const modelosFiltrados = modelos.filter(
    (modelo) => String(modelo.marcaVehi) === String(form.marca),
  );

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setWarning('');

    const incompleto = Object.values(form).some((value) => !String(value).trim());
    if (incompleto) {
      setWarning('Todos los campos son obligatorios.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidEmail(form.email)) {
      setWarning('El e-mail no tiene un formato válido.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidPatente(form.patente)) {
      setWarning('La patente debe tener el formato AA-123-AA.');
      return;
    }

    const dni = String(form.dni).trim();
    const existentes = await searchClientes(dni);
    const yaExiste = existentes.some((cliente) => String(cliente.dni) === dni);

    if (yaExiste) {
      setPopup({ message: 'Ese cliente ya se encuentra registrado.', action: 'clear' });
      return;
    }

    try {
      const cliente = await createCliente({
        nombre: form.nombre,
        apellido: form.apellido,
        dni: form.dni,
        email: form.email,
        celular: form.celular,
      });
      await createVehiculo({
        patenteVehi: form.patente,
        estadoVehi: true,
        cliente: cliente.codigoPersona,
        modeloVehi: form.modelo,
      });
      setPopup({ message: `Bienvenido ${form.nombre}`, action: 'navigate' });
    } catch {
      setWarning('No se pudo registrar el cliente.');
    }
  }

  function handlePopupClose() {
    const action = popup?.action;
    setPopup(null);
    if (action === 'clear') {
      setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
    } else if (action === 'navigate') {
      navigate('/clientes');
    }
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  const inputClass =
    'mt-1 w-full rounded-full bg-brand-input px-4 py-2 text-white outline-none';
  const labelClass = 'block text-sm font-bold text-white';

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl bg-brand-pink p-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#5ea014]">Nuevo Cliente</h1>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-brand-indigo p-4">
            <div className="mb-3 flex justify-center">
              <img src={userIcon} alt="Usuario" className="h-12 w-12" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="dni" className={labelClass}>
                  DNI
                </label>
                <input
                  id="dni"
                  name="dni"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.dni}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('dni', onlyDigits(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="nombre" className={labelClass}>
                  Nombre
                </label>
                <input
                  id="nombre"
                  name="nombre"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.nombre}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('nombre', onlyLetters(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="apellido" className={labelClass}>
                  Apellido
                </label>
                <input
                  id="apellido"
                  name="apellido"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.apellido}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('apellido', onlyLetters(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="celular" className={labelClass}>
                  Celular
                </label>
                <input
                  id="celular"
                  name="celular"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.celular}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('celular', onlyDigits(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="email" className={labelClass}>
                  e-mail
                </label>
                <input
                  id="email"
                  name="email"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.email}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('email', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-brand-indigo p-4">
            <div className="mb-3 flex justify-center">
              <img src={autoIcon} alt="Vehiculo" className="h-10 w-10" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="categoria" className={labelClass}>
                  Tipo Vehiculo
                </label>
                <select
                  id="categoria"
                  name="categoria"
                  value={form.categoria}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('categoria', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {categorias.map((categoria) => (
                    <option key={categoria.codigoCategoriaVehi} value={categoria.codigoCategoriaVehi}>
                      {categoria.categoriaVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="marca" className={labelClass}>
                  Marca Vehiculo
                </label>
                <select
                  id="marca"
                  name="marca"
                  value={form.marca}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('marca', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {marcasFiltradas.map((marca) => (
                    <option key={marca.codigoMarcaVehi} value={marca.codigoMarcaVehi}>
                      {marca.marcaVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="modelo" className={labelClass}>
                  Modelo Vehiculo
                </label>
                <select
                  id="modelo"
                  name="modelo"
                  value={form.modelo}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('modelo', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {modelosFiltrados.map((modelo) => (
                    <option key={modelo.codigoModeloVehi} value={modelo.codigoModeloVehi}>
                      {modelo.modeloVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="patente" className={labelClass}>
                  Patente
                </label>
                <input
                  id="patente"
                  name="patente"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.patente}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('patente', formatPatente(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {warning ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
              {warning}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-6">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-full bg-[#5ea014] px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Registrar
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate('/clientes')}
              className="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {popup ? <Popup message={popup.message} onClose={handlePopupClose} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoClientePage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoClientePage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import NuevoClientePage from './NuevoClientePage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let categorias = [];
let marcas = [];
let modelos = [];
let searchResult = [];

function mockFetch(url) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/categorias-vehiculo') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: categorias }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/marcas-vehiculo') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: marcas }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/modelos-vehiculo') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: modelos }) });
  }
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/clientes?buscar=')) {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: searchResult }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/clientes') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: { codigoPersona: 5 } }) });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: { codigoVehi: 9 } }) });
  }
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [] }) });
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/clientes/nuevo']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/clientes/nuevo" element={<NuevoClientePage />} />
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<div>CLIENTES PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

async function fillForm(user, overrides = {}) {
  const values = {
    dni: '12345678',
    nombre: 'Juan',
    apellido: 'Perez',
    celular: '5551234',
    email: 'juan@example.com',
    patente: 'AA123BB',
    ...overrides,
  };

  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('DNI'), values.dni);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'), values.nombre);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Apellido'), values.apellido);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Celular'), values.celular);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('e-mail'), values.email);
  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Tipo Vehiculo'), '1');
  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Marca Vehiculo'), '10');
  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Modelo Vehiculo'), '100');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'), values.patente);
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  categorias = [{ codigoCategoriaVehi: 1, categoriaVehi: 'Auto' }];
  marcas = [{ codigoMarcaVehi: 10, marcaVehi: 'Fiat', categoriaVehi: 1 }];
  modelos = [{ codigoModeloVehi: 100, modeloVehi: 'Uno', marcaVehi: 10 }];
  searchResult = [];
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('NuevoClientePage', () => {
  test('muestra el título, los campos y los botones', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('DNI')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Apellido')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Celular')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('e-mail')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Tipo Vehiculo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Marca Vehiculo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Modelo Vehiculo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Patente')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('con un campo vacío advierte y no registra', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/clientes', expect.anything());
  });

  test('las marcas dependen del tipo y los modelos de la marca', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    expect(screen.queryByRole('option', { name: 'Fiat' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Tipo Vehiculo'), '1');
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Fiat' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.queryByRole('option', { name: 'Uno' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Marca Vehiculo'), '10');
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Uno' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('con dni ya registrado muestra el popup y limpia el formulario', async () => {
    searchResult = [{ codigoPersona: 1, dni: 12345678, nombre: 'Juan' }];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await fillForm(user);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Ese cliente ya se encuentra registrado.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('DNI')).toHaveValue('');
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/clientes', expect.anything());
  });

  test('registra el cliente y el vehículo y muestra Bienvenido', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await fillForm(user);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Bienvenido Juan')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTES PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Cancelar navega a la página de Clientes', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTES PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('la patente se formatea mientras se tipea', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'), 'ab254mc');

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Patente')).toHaveValue('AB-254-MC');
  });

  test('DNI y Celular aceptan solo números', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('DNI'), '12a3');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Celular'), '55b5');

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('DNI')).toHaveValue('123');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Celular')).toHaveValue('555');
  });

  test('Nombre y Apellido aceptan solo letras (con acentos y ñ)', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre'), 'Muñoz1');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Apellido'), 'Pérez2');

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Nombre')).toHaveValue('Muñoz');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Apellido')).toHaveValue('Pérez');
  });

  test('con e-mail inválido advierte y no registra', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await fillForm(user, { email: 'juan@' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('El e-mail no tiene un formato válido.');
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/clientes', expect.anything());
  });

  test('con patente inválida advierte y no registra', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Cliente');

    await fillForm(user, { patente: 'AB254M' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'La patente debe tener el formato AA-123-AA.',
    );
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/clientes', expect.anything());
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoClientePage.test.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoLavadoPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import BrandHeader from '../components/BrandHeader.jsx';
import ConfirmPopup from '../components/ConfirmPopup.jsx';
import { fetchSessionData } from '../services/authService.js';
import {
  createLavado,
  listCategoriasLavado,
  listEstadosLavado,
  listUtileria,
  updateUtileria,
} from '../services/lavadoService.js';
import { getVehiculo } from '../services/vehicleService.js';

function codigo(valor) {
  return String(valor);
}

export default function NuevoLavadoPage() {
  const { id, vehiculoId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [vehiculo, setVehiculo] = useState(null);
  const [utilerias, setUtilerias] = useState([]);
  const [categorias, setCategorias] = useState([]);
  const [estados, setEstados] = useState([]);
  const [seleccion, setSeleccion] = useState([]);
  const [categoria, setCategoria] = useState('');
  const [estado, setEstado] = useState('');
  const [detalles, setDetalles] = useState('');
  const [login, setLogin] = useState(null);
  const [warning, setWarning] = useState('');
  const [showConfirm, setShowConfirm] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSessionData()
      .then((session) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!session) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        setLogin(session.codigoLogin);

        Promise.all([
          getVehiculo(vehiculoId),
          listUtileria(),
          listCategoriasLavado(),
          listEstadosLavado(),
        ]).then(([datosVehiculo, listaUtileria, listaCategorias, listaEstados]) => {
          if (!active) {
            return;
          }
          setVehiculo(datosVehiculo);
          setUtilerias(listaUtileria);
          setCategorias(listaCategorias);
          setEstados(listaEstados);
          setSeleccion(
            listaUtileria
              .filter((utileria) => Boolean(utileria.estadoUtileria))
              .map((utileria) => codigo(utileria.codigoUtileria)),
          );
          setChecking(false);
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [vehiculoId, navigate]);

  async function toggleUtileria(codigoUtileria) {
    const activa = seleccion.includes(codigoUtileria);
    const nuevoEstado = !activa;

    try {
      await updateUtileria(codigoUtileria, { estadoUtileria: nuevoEstado });
      setSeleccion((prev) =>
        nuevoEstado ? [...prev, codigoUtileria] : prev.filter((item) => item !== codigoUtileria),
      );
      setWarning('');
    } catch {
      setWarning('No se pudo actualizar la utileria.');
    }
  }

  function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setWarning('');

    if (!categoria) {
      setWarning('Seleccione una categoria.');
      return;
    }
    if (!estado) {
      setWarning('Seleccione un estado.');
      return;
    }
    if (seleccion.length === 0) {
      setWarning('Seleccione al menos una utileria.');
      return;
    }

    setShowConfirm(true);
  }

  async function handleConfirm() {
    try {
      await createLavado({
        vehi: vehiculoId,
        categoriaLavado: categoria,
        estado,
        utileria: seleccion[0],
        detallesLavado: detalles,
        login,
      });
      setShowConfirm(false);
      navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`);
    } catch {
      setShowConfirm(false);
      setWarning('No se pudo registrar el lavado.');
    }
  }

  function handleCancelConfirm() {
    setShowConfirm(false);
    setCategoria('');
    setEstado('');
    setDetalles('');
    setSeleccion(
      utilerias
        .filter((utileria) => Boolean(utileria.estadoUtileria))
        .map((utileria) => codigo(utileria.codigoUtileria)),
    );
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  const labelClass = 'block text-sm font-bold text-white';

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <BrandHeader />

      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border-3 border-blue-900 bg-[#8f8a8a] p-6">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-navy">
          Nuevo Lavado: <span className="font-extrabold">{vehiculo?.patenteVehi}</span>
        </h1>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-[#2637a2] p-6">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-8">
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
              {utilerias.map((utileria) => {
                const valor = codigo(utileria.codigoUtileria);
                return (
                  <label
                    key={valor}
                    className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                  >
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={seleccion.includes(valor)}
                      onChange={() => toggleUtileria(valor)}
                      className="h-5 w-5"
                    />
                    {utileria.descripcionUtileria}
                  </label>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
              {categorias.map((item) => {
                const valor = codigo(item.codigoCategoriaLavado);
                const activa = categoria === valor;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={valor}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
                  >
                    <span>{item.descripcionLavado}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      role="switch"
                      aria-checked={activa}
                      aria-label={item.descripcionLavado}
                      onClick={() => setCategoria(valor)}
                      className={`h-6 w-12 rounded-full p-1 transition-colors ${
                        activa ? 'bg-green-600' : 'bg-red-600'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span
                        className={`block h-4 w-4 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform ${
                          activa ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-0'
                        }`}
                      />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6">
            <label htmlFor="detalles" className={labelClass}>
              Detalles
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="detalles"
              value={detalles}
              onChange={(event) => setDetalles(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Detalles..."
              rows={3}
              className="mt-1 w-full rounded-2xl bg-brand-input px-4 py-2 text-white outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-white px-4 py-2">
              <label htmlFor="estado" className={labelClass}>
                Estado...
              </label>
              <select
                id="estado"
                value={estado}
                onChange={(event) => setEstado(event.target.value)}
                className="rounded-md bg-white px-3 py-1 text-brand-navy outline-none"
              >
                <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                {estados.map((item) => (
                  <option key={item.codigoEstado} value={item.codigoEstado}>
                    {item.descripcionEstado}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {warning ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-center text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
              {warning}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-6">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Registrar Lavado
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`)}
              className="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {showConfirm ? (
        <ConfirmPopup
          message={`¿ Desea registrar el lavado del vehiculo "${vehiculo?.patenteVehi ?? ''}" ?`}
          onConfirm={handleConfirm}
          onCancel={handleCancelConfirm}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoLavadoPage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoLavadoPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import NuevoLavadoPage from './NuevoLavadoPage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let utilerias = [];
let categorias = [];
let estados = [];
let postBody = null;

function data(value) {
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: value }) });
}

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: sessionOk,
      status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: sessionOk ? { codigoLogin: 3 } : null }),
    });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos/9') {
    return data({ codigoVehi: 9, patenteVehi: 'AC-125-BF', cliente: 5 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/utilerias') {
    return data(utilerias);
  }
  if (url === '/api/categorias-lavado') {
    return data(categorias);
  }
  if (url === '/api/estados-lavado') {
    return data(estados);
  }
  if (typeof url === 'string' && url.startsWith('/api/utilerias/')) {
    return data({ estadoUtileria: true });
  }
  if (url === '/api/lavados' && options.method === 'POST') {
    postBody = JSON.parse(options.body);
    return data({ codigoLavado: 1 });
  }
  return data([]);
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/clientes/5/vehiculos/9/lavar']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/:vehiculoId/lavar" element={<NuevoLavadoPage />} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/info" element={<div>CLIENTE INFO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  utilerias = [
    { codigoUtileria: 1, descripcionUtileria: 'Auxilio', estadoUtileria: false },
    { codigoUtileria: 2, descripcionUtileria: 'Balizas', estadoUtileria: false },
  ];
  categorias = [
    { codigoCategoriaLavado: 10, descripcionLavado: 'Basico' },
    { codigoCategoriaLavado: 11, descripcionLavado: 'Completo' },
  ];
  estados = [{ codigoEstado: 20, descripcionEstado: 'En Espera' }];
  postBody = null;
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('NuevoLavadoPage', () => {
  test('muestra la patente y los catálogos', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('AC-125-BF')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Detalles')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Estado...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar Lavado' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sólo un toggle de categoría queda encendido', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('AC-125-BF');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Completo' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Completo' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
  });

  test('tildar una utilería actualiza su estado', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('AC-125-BF');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/utilerias/1',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT' }),
    );
  });

  test('exige categoría, estado y utilería', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('AC-125-BF');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar Lavado' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Seleccione una categoria.');
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/lavados',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
  });

  test('registra el lavado con confirmación y vuelve a la información', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('AC-125-BF');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' }));
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Estado...'), '20');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Detalles'), 'Lavado 1');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar Lavado' }));

    expect(
      screen.getByText('¿ Desea registrar el lavado del vehiculo "AC-125-BF" ?'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' }));

    expect(postBody).toEqual({
      vehi: '9',
      categoriaLavado: '10',
      estado: '20',
      utileria: '1',
      detallesLavado: 'Lavado 1',
      login: 3,
    });
    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTE INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('cancelar en el popup restablece el formulario sin registrar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('AC-125-BF');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Auxilio' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' }));
    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Estado...'), '20');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Detalles'), 'algo');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar Lavado' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'CANCELAR' }));

    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/lavados',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Detalles')).toHaveValue('');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Estado...')).toHaveValue('');
    expect(screen.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Basico' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
  });

  test('Cancelar vuelve a la información sin registrar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('AC-125-BF');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTE INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoLavadoPage.test.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoVehiculoPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import autoIcon from '../assets/auto.png';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import {
  createVehiculo,
  listCategorias,
  listMarcas,
  listModelos,
  listVehiculos,
} from '../services/vehicleService.js';
import { formatPatente, isValidPatente } from '../utils/validators.js';

const EMPTY_FORM = {
  categoria: '',
  marca: '',
  modelo: '',
  patente: '',
};

export default function NuevoVehiculoPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM);
  const [categorias, setCategorias] = useState([]);
  const [marcas, setMarcas] = useState([]);
  const [modelos, setModelos] = useState([]);
  const [warning, setWarning] = useState('');
  const [popup, setPopup] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        } else {
          setChecking(false);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [navigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    listCategorias().then(setCategorias);
    listMarcas().then(setMarcas);
    listModelos().then(setModelos);
  }, []);

  function updateField(name, value) {
    setForm((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev, [name]: value };
      if (name === 'categoria') {
        next.marca = '';
        next.modelo = '';
      }
      if (name === 'marca') {
        next.modelo = '';
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  const marcasFiltradas = marcas.filter(
    (marca) => String(marca.categoriaVehi) === String(form.categoria),
  );
  const modelosFiltrados = modelos.filter(
    (modelo) => String(modelo.marcaVehi) === String(form.marca),
  );

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setWarning('');

    const incompleto = Object.values(form).some((value) => !String(value).trim());
    if (incompleto) {
      setWarning('Todos los campos son obligatorios.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isValidPatente(form.patente)) {
      setWarning('La patente debe tener el formato AA-123-AA.');
      return;
    }

    const patente = form.patente.trim().toUpperCase();
    const existentes = await listVehiculos();
    const yaExiste = existentes.some(
      (vehiculo) => String(vehiculo.patenteVehi).trim().toUpperCase() === patente,
    );

    if (yaExiste) {
      setPopup({ message: 'Vehiculo ya registrado :/', action: 'clear' });
      return;
    }

    try {
      await createVehiculo({
        patenteVehi: form.patente,
        estadoVehi: true,
        cliente: id,
        modeloVehi: form.modelo,
      });
      setPopup({ message: 'Vehiculo agregado satisfactoriamente.', action: 'navigate' });
    } catch {
      setWarning('No se pudo registrar el vehiculo.');
    }
  }

  function handlePopupClose() {
    const action = popup?.action;
    setPopup(null);
    if (action === 'clear') {
      setForm(EMPTY_FORM);
    } else if (action === 'navigate') {
      navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`);
    }
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  const inputClass =
    'mt-1 w-full rounded-full bg-brand-input px-4 py-2 text-white outline-none';
  const labelClass = 'block text-sm font-bold text-white';

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl bg-brand-pink p-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#5ea014]">Nuevo Vehiculo</h1>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl bg-brand-indigo p-4">
            <div className="mb-3 flex justify-center">
              <img src={autoIcon} alt="Vehiculo" className="h-10 w-10" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="categoria" className={labelClass}>
                  Vehiculo
                </label>
                <select
                  id="categoria"
                  name="categoria"
                  value={form.categoria}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('categoria', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {categorias.map((categoria) => (
                    <option key={categoria.codigoCategoriaVehi} value={categoria.codigoCategoriaVehi}>
                      {categoria.categoriaVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="marca" className={labelClass}>
                  Marca
                </label>
                <select
                  id="marca"
                  name="marca"
                  value={form.marca}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('marca', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {marcasFiltradas.map((marca) => (
                    <option key={marca.codigoMarcaVehi} value={marca.codigoMarcaVehi}>
                      {marca.marcaVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="modelo" className={labelClass}>
                  Modelo
                </label>
                <select
                  id="modelo"
                  name="modelo"
                  value={form.modelo}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('modelo', event.target.value)}
                  className={inputClass}
                >
                  <option value="">Seleccionar...</option>
                  {modelosFiltrados.map((modelo) => (
                    <option key={modelo.codigoModeloVehi} value={modelo.codigoModeloVehi}>
                      {modelo.modeloVehi}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="patente" className={labelClass}>
                  Patente
                </label>
                <input
                  id="patente"
                  name="patente"
                  type="text"
                  value={form.patente}
                  onChange={(event) => updateField('patente', formatPatente(event.target.value))}
                  className={inputClass}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {warning ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
              {warning}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-6">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-full bg-[#5ea014] px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Registrar
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate(`/clientes/${id}/info`)}
              className="rounded-full bg-red-600 px-8 py-2 font-bold text-white"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      {popup ? <Popup message={popup.message} onClose={handlePopupClose} /> : null}
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoVehiculoPage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/NuevoVehiculoPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import NuevoVehiculoPage from './NuevoVehiculoPage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let categorias = [];
let marcas = [];
let modelos = [];
let vehiculos = [];
let postOk = true;
let postBody = null;

function data(value) {
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: value }) });
}

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/categorias-vehiculo') {
    return data(categorias);
  }
  if (url === '/api/marcas-vehiculo') {
    return data(marcas);
  }
  if (url === '/api/modelos-vehiculo') {
    return data(modelos);
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos' && options.method === 'POST') {
    postBody = JSON.parse(options.body);
    return Promise.resolve(
      postOk
        ? { ok: true, status: 201, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: { codigoVehi: 9 } }) }
        : { ok: false, status: 400 },
    );
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos') {
    return data(vehiculos);
  }
  return data([]);
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/clientes/5/vehiculos/nuevo']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/nuevo" element={<NuevoVehiculoPage />} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/info" element={<div>CLIENTE INFO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

async function fillForm(user, overrides = {}) {
  const values = { patente: 'AA123BB', ...overrides };

  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Vehiculo'), '1');
  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Marca'), '10');
  await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Modelo'), '100');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'), values.patente);
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  categorias = [{ codigoCategoriaVehi: 1, categoriaVehi: 'Auto' }];
  marcas = [{ codigoMarcaVehi: 10, marcaVehi: 'Fiat', categoriaVehi: 1 }];
  modelos = [{ codigoModeloVehi: 100, modeloVehi: 'Uno', marcaVehi: 10 }];
  vehiculos = [];
  postOk = true;
  postBody = null;
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('NuevoVehiculoPage', () => {
  test('muestra el título, los campos y los botones', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Vehiculo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Marca')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Modelo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Patente')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('las marcas dependen del vehículo y los modelos de la marca', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    expect(screen.queryByRole('option', { name: 'Fiat' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Vehiculo'), '1');
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Fiat' })).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.queryByRole('option', { name: 'Uno' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.selectOptions(screen.getByLabelText('Marca'), '10');
    expect(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Uno' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('con un campo vacío advierte y no registra', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Todos los campos son obligatorios.');
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
  });

  test('con patente inválida advierte y no registra', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    await fillForm(user, { patente: 'AB254M' });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'La patente debe tener el formato AA-123-AA.',
    );
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
  });

  test('con patente ya registrada muestra el popup y limpia el formulario', async () => {
    vehiculos = [{ codigoVehi: 1, patenteVehi: 'AA-123-BB' }];
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    await fillForm(user);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Vehiculo ya registrado :/')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Vehiculo')).toHaveValue('');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Marca')).toHaveValue('');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Modelo')).toHaveValue('');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Patente')).toHaveValue('');
  });

  test('registra el vehículo, muestra el popup y vuelve a la información', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    await fillForm(user);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('Vehiculo agregado satisfactoriamente.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(postBody).toEqual({
      patenteVehi: 'AA-123-BB',
      estadoVehi: true,
      cliente: '5',
      modeloVehi: '100',
    });

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aceptar' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTE INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('ante un error de registro advierte y no navega', async () => {
    postOk = false;
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    await fillForm(user);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Registrar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent(
      'No se pudo registrar el vehiculo.',
    );
    expect(screen.queryByText('CLIENTE INFO PAGE')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('Cancelar vuelve a la información del cliente sin registrar', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancelar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTE INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(globalThis.fetch).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/vehiculos',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST' }),
    );
  });

  test('la patente se formatea mientras se tipea', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByText('Nuevo Vehiculo');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Patente'), 'ab254mc');

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Patente')).toHaveValue('AB-254-MC');
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/VehiculoInfoPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import ClienteVehiculoInfo from '../components/ClienteVehiculoInfo.jsx';
import ConfirmPopup from '../components/ConfirmPopup.jsx';
import Popup from '../components/Popup.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { deleteCliente } from '../services/clientService.js';
import { MENSAJE_LAVADO_ACTIVO, tieneLavadoActivo } from '../services/lavadoService.js';
import { deleteVehiculo, getVehiculo, listByCliente } from '../services/vehicleService.js';

function toCliente(vehiculo) {
  const persona = vehiculo?.datosCliente?.persona ?? {};
  return {
    codigoPersona: vehiculo?.cliente ?? vehiculo?.datosCliente?.codigoPersona,
    dni: persona.dni,
    nombre: persona.nombre,
    apellido: persona.apellido,
    celular: persona.celular,
    email: persona.email,
  };
}

export default function VehiculoInfoPage() {
  const { vehiculoId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [vehiculo, setVehiculo] = useState(null);
  const [cliente, setCliente] = useState(null);
  const [showDeleteCliente, setShowDeleteCliente] = useState(false);
  const [showDeleteVehiculo, setShowDeleteVehiculo] = useState(false);
  const [showBloqueo, setShowBloqueo] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        getVehiculo(vehiculoId).then((datos) => {
          if (!active) {
            return;
          }
          setVehiculo(datos);
          setCliente(toCliente(datos));
          setChecking(false);
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [vehiculoId, navigate]);

  async function clienteBloqueado() {
    const lista = await listByCliente(cliente?.codigoPersona);
    return tieneLavadoActivo(lista.map((item) => item.codigoVehi));
  }

  async function handleEditarCliente() {
    if (await clienteBloqueado()) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    navigate(`/clientes/${cliente?.codigoPersona}/editar`);
  }

  async function handleEliminarCliente() {
    if (await clienteBloqueado()) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    setShowDeleteCliente(true);
  }

  async function handleEditarVehiculo(item) {
    if (await tieneLavadoActivo([item.codigoVehi])) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    navigate(`/clientes/${cliente?.codigoPersona}/vehiculos/${item.codigoVehi}/editar`);
  }

  async function handleEliminarVehiculo(item) {
    if (await tieneLavadoActivo([item.codigoVehi])) {
      setShowBloqueo(true);
      return;
    }
    setShowDeleteVehiculo(true);
  }

  async function handleConfirmDeleteCliente() {
    try {
      await deleteCliente(cliente?.codigoPersona);
      navigate('/clientes');
    } catch (error) {
      setShowDeleteCliente(false);
      if (error.status === 409) {
        setShowBloqueo(true);
        return;
      }
      navigate('/clientes');
    }
  }

  async function handleConfirmDeleteVehiculo() {
    try {
      await deleteVehiculo(vehiculoId);
      navigate('/vehiculos');
    } catch (error) {
      setShowDeleteVehiculo(false);
      if (error.status === 409) {
        setShowBloqueo(true);
        return;
      }
      navigate('/vehiculos');
    }
  }

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <ClienteVehiculoInfo
        cliente={cliente}
        vehiculos={vehiculo ? [vehiculo] : []}
        onNuevoVehiculo={() => navigate(`/clientes/${cliente?.codigoPersona}/vehiculos/nuevo`)}
        onEditarCliente={handleEditarCliente}
        onEliminarCliente={handleEliminarCliente}
        onEditarVehiculo={handleEditarVehiculo}
        onEliminarVehiculo={handleEliminarVehiculo}
        onVolver={() => navigate('/vehiculos')}
      />

      {showDeleteCliente ? (
        <ConfirmPopup
          message={`¿ Desea eliminar al cliente "${cliente?.nombre ?? ''} ${cliente?.apellido ?? ''}" ?`}
          onConfirm={handleConfirmDeleteCliente}
          onCancel={() => setShowDeleteCliente(false)}
        />
      ) : null}

      {showDeleteVehiculo ? (
        <ConfirmPopup
          message={`¿ Desea eliminar el vehiculo "${vehiculo?.patenteVehi ?? ''}" ?`}
          onConfirm={handleConfirmDeleteVehiculo}
          onCancel={() => setShowDeleteVehiculo(false)}
        />
      ) : null}

      {showBloqueo ? (
        <Popup message={MENSAJE_LAVADO_ACTIVO} onClose={() => setShowBloqueo(false)} />
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/VehiculoInfoPage.jsx =====

import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import VehiculoInfoPage from './VehiculoInfoPage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let lavadosVehiculo = [];
let vehiculosCliente = [];
let deleteStatus = 204;

const vehiculo = {
  codigoVehi: 9,
  patenteVehi: 'AB-122-FC',
  cliente: 5,
  modelo: {
    codigoModeloVehi: 100,
    modeloVehi: 'Picasso',
    marca: {
      codigoMarcaVehi: 10,
      marcaVehi: 'Citroen',
      categoria: { codigoCategoriaVehi: 1, categoriaVehi: 'Automovil' },
    },
  },
  datosCliente: {
    codigoPersona: 5,
    estadoCliente: true,
    persona: {
      dni: 28853432,
      nombre: 'Juan',
      apellido: 'Perez',
      celular: 555,
      email: 'juan@gmail.com',
    },
  },
};

function mockFetch(url, options = {}) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos/9' && options.method === 'DELETE') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: deleteStatus < 400, status: deleteStatus });
  }
  if (url === '/api/clientes/5' && options.method === 'DELETE') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: deleteStatus < 400, status: deleteStatus });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos?cliente=5') {
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: true,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: vehiculosCliente }),
    });
  }
  if (url === '/api/lavados?vehi=9') {
    return Promise.resolve({
      ok: true,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: lavadosVehiculo }),
    });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos/9') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: vehiculo }) });
  }
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [] }) });
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/vehiculos/9/info']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/vehiculos/:vehiculoId/info" element={<VehiculoInfoPage />} />
        <Route path="/vehiculos" element={<div>VEHICULOS PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<div>CLIENTES PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/editar" element={<div>EDITAR CLIENTE PAGE</div>} />
        <Route
          path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/:vehiculoId/editar"
          element={<div>EDITAR VEHICULO PAGE</div>}
        />
        <Route path="/clientes/:id/vehiculos/nuevo" element={<div>NUEVO VEHICULO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  lavadosVehiculo = [];
  vehiculosCliente = [];
  deleteStatus = 204;
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('VehiculoInfoPage', () => {
  test('muestra el vehículo y los datos de su cliente', async () => {

    expect(screen.getByText('Perez')).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(screen.getByText('Picasso')).toBeInTheDocument();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();

  test('Volver navega a Vehiculos', async () => {

    const card = await screen.findByTestId('cliente-card');
    await user.click(within(card).getByRole('button', { name: 'Editar' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('EDITAR CLIENTE PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();

  });

  test('Editar del vehículo con lavado activo muestra el aviso y no navega', async () => {

    expect(
      await screen.findByText('Edicion / Eliminacion cancelada...Vehiculo en estado de Lavado'),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('EDITAR VEHICULO PAGE')).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'ACEPTAR' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    lavadosVehiculo = [{ datosEstado: { descripcionEstado: 'Terminado' } }];

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/VehiculosPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import AppHeader from '../components/AppHeader.jsx';
import { fetchSession } from '../services/authService.js';
import { listVehiculos } from '../services/vehicleService.js';

const COLUMNS = ['TIPO', 'MARCA', 'MODELO', 'PATENTE'];
const ROW_COLORS = ['#f4e6e6', '#b4b2b2'];

function matches(vehiculo, termino) {
  const texto = termino.trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!texto) {
    return false;
  }
  const patente = String(vehiculo.patenteVehi ?? '').toUpperCase();
  const modelo = String(vehiculo.modelo?.modeloVehi ?? '').toUpperCase();
  return patente.includes(texto) || modelo.includes(texto);
}

export default function VehiculosPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
  const [term, setTerm] = useState('');
  const [vehiculos, setVehiculos] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetchSession()
      .then((ok) => {
        if (!active) {
          return;
        }
        if (!ok) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
          return;
        }
        listVehiculos().then((lista) => {
          if (active) {
            setVehiculos(lista);
            setChecking(false);
          }
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) {
          navigate('/login', { replace: true });
        }
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [navigate]);

  if (checking) {
    return null;
  }

  const resultados = vehiculos.filter((vehiculo) => matches(vehiculo, term));

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-brand-indigo p-6">
      <AppHeader />

      <div className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border-4 border-gray-500 p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="inline-block rounded-full bg-[#2637a2] px-6 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-white">
            VEHICULOS
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate('/clientes')}
            className="rounded-full bg-green-600 px-6 py-2 font-bold text-white"
          >
            + Vehiculo
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mx-auto mt-4 flex max-w-2xl items-center rounded-2xl bg-[#8f8a8a] p-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={term}
            onChange={(event) => setTerm(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Patente..."
            aria-label="Buscar vehiculos"
            className="flex-1 rounded-full bg-[#f4e6e6] px-4 py-2 text-brand-navy outline-none"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-2xl rounded-2xl bg-[#2637a2] p-4">
          <table className="w-full text-center">
            <thead>
              <tr className="text-gray-400">
                {COLUMNS.map((col) => (
                  <th key={col} className="p-2 text-sm font-bold">
                    {col}
                  </th>
                ))}
                <th className="p-2" />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {resultados.length > 0 ? (
                resultados.map((vehiculo, index) => (
                  <tr
                    key={vehiculo.codigoVehi}
                    data-testid="vehiculo-row"
                    style={{ backgroundColor: ROW_COLORS[index % ROW_COLORS.length] }}
                  >
                    <td className="p-2">{vehiculo.modelo?.marca?.categoria?.categoriaVehi}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">{vehiculo.modelo?.marca?.marcaVehi}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">{vehiculo.modelo?.modeloVehi}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">{vehiculo.patenteVehi}</td>
                    <td className="p-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => navigate(`/vehiculos/${vehiculo.codigoVehi}/info`)}
                        className="rounded-full bg-[#7eb93c] px-4 py-1 font-bold text-white"
                      >
                        <span className="text-lg">+</span> info
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              ) : (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={COLUMNS.length + 1} className="p-6 text-center font-bold text-gray-400">
                    Busqueda sin resultados ...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate('/home')}
            className="rounded-2xl bg-green-600 px-4 py-2 text-xl font-bold text-white"
          >
            ← <span className="text-lg">Volver</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== FIN ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/VehiculosPage.jsx =====

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/pages/VehiculosPage.test.jsx =====
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import VehiculosPage from './VehiculosPage.jsx';

let sessionOk = true;
let vehiculos = [];

function baseVehiculos() {
  return [
    {
      codigoVehi: 1,
      patenteVehi: 'AB-122-FC',
      modelo: {
        modeloVehi: 'Picasso',
        marca: { marcaVehi: 'Citroen', categoria: { categoriaVehi: 'Automovil' } },
      },
    },
    {
      codigoVehi: 2,
      patenteVehi: 'XY-999-ZZ',
      modelo: {
        modeloVehi: 'Corolla',
        marca: { marcaVehi: 'Toyota', categoria: { categoriaVehi: 'Automovil' } },
      },
    },
  ];
}

function mockFetch(url) {
  if (url === '/api/auth/session') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: sessionOk, status: sessionOk ? 200 : 401 });
  }
  if (url === '/api/vehiculos') {
    return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: vehiculos }) });
  }
  return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [] }) });
}

function renderPage() {
  return render(
    <MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/vehiculos']}>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/vehiculos" element={<VehiculosPage />} />
        <Route path="/vehiculos/:vehiculoId/info" element={<div>VEHICULO INFO PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/clientes" element={<div>CLIENTES PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/home" element={<div>HOME PAGE</div>} />
        <Route path="/login" element={<div>LOGIN PAGE</div>} />
      </Routes>
    </MemoryRouter>,
  );
}

beforeEach(() => {
  sessionOk = true;
  vehiculos = baseVehiculos();
  globalThis.fetch = vi.fn(mockFetch);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('VehiculosPage', () => {
  test('muestra el pill, el buscador y la tabla', async () => {
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Patente...')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('VEHICULOS')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('TIPO')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('MARCA')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('MODELO')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('PATENTE')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '← Volver' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin sesión válida redirige al login', async () => {
    sessionOk = false;
    renderPage();

    expect(await screen.findByText('LOGIN PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin parámetros no muestra vehículos', async () => {
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    expect(screen.queryAllByTestId('vehiculo-row')).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(screen.getByText('Busqueda sin resultados ...')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('busca por parte de la patente', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos'), '122');

    expect(await screen.findByText('AB-122-FC')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('XY-999-ZZ')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('busca por parte del modelo', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos'), 'corolla');

    expect(await screen.findByText('XY-999-ZZ')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('AB-122-FC')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('sin coincidencias muestra "Busqueda sin resultados ..."', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos'), 'zzz');

    expect(screen.getByText('Busqueda sin resultados ...')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('muestra filas alternadas y un botón + info por fila', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos'), 'o');

    const rows = await screen.findAllByTestId('vehiculo-row');
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(rows[0].style.backgroundColor).toContain('244');
    expect(rows[1].style.backgroundColor).toContain('180');
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: '+ info' })).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  test('+ info navega a la página del vehículo', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos'), '122');
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: '+ info' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('VEHICULO INFO PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  test('+ Vehiculo navega a la página de Clientes', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '+ Vehiculo' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('CLIENTES PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  test('Volver navega al home', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderPage();
    await screen.findByLabelText('Buscar vehiculos');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '← Volver' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText('HOME PAGE')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

===== INICIO ARCHIVO: src/frontend/src/services/authService.js =====
export async function login(usuario, contrasena) {
  const response = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    credentials: 'include',
    body: JSON.stringify({ usuario, contrasena }),
  });

  return response.ok;
}

export async function fetchSession() {
  const response = await fetch('/api/auth/session', {
    credentials: 'include',
  });

  return response.ok;
}

export async function fetchSessionData() {
  const response = await fetch('/api/auth/session', {
    credentials: 'include',
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    return null;
  }

  const body = await response.json();
  return body.data ?? null;
}

export async function logout() {
  await fetch('/api/auth/logout', {
    method: 'POST',
    credentials: 'include',
  });
}

export async function searchClientes(texto) {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/clientes?buscar=${encodeURIComponent(texto)}`, {
    credentials: 'include',
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    return [];
  }

  const body = await response.json();
  return body.data ?? [];
}

export async function listClientesSinLavados(desde) {
  const response = await fetch(
    `/api/clientes?sinLavadosDesde=${encodeURIComponent(desde)}`,
    { credentials: 'include' },
  );

  if (!response.ok) {
    return [];
  }

  const body = await response.json();
  return body.data ?? [];
}

export async function getCliente(id) {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/clientes/${id}`, { credentials: 'include' });

  if (!response.ok) {
    return null;
  }

  const body = await response.json();
  return body.data;
}

export async function deleteCliente(id) {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/clientes/${id}`, {
    method: 'DELETE',
    credentials: 'include',
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error('No se pudo eliminar el cliente.');
    error.status = response.status;
    throw error;
  }
}

export async function updateCliente(id, datos) {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/clientes/${id}`, {
    method: 'PUT',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    credentials: 'include',
    body: JSON.stringify(datos),
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error('No se pudo actualizar el cliente.');
  }

  const body = await response.json();
  return body.data;
}

export async function createCliente(cliente) {
  const response = await fetch('/api/clientes', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    credentials: 'include',
    body: JSON.stringify(cliente),
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error('No se pudo crear el cliente.');
  }

  const body = await response.json();
  return body.data;
}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import {
  createCliente,
  deleteCliente,
  getCliente,
  listClientesSinLavados,
  searchClientes,
  updateCliente,
} from './clientService.js';

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('clientService', () => {
  test('searchClientes consulta GET /api/clientes?buscar= con credentials include', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [{ codigoPersona: 1 }] }),
    });

    const data = await searchClientes('juan');

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/clientes?buscar=juan', {
      credentials: 'include',
    });
    expect(data).toEqual([{ codigoPersona: 1 }]);
  });

  test('listClientesSinLavados consulta GET /api/clientes?sinLavadosDesde=', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: [{ codigoPersona: 1 }] }),
    });

    const data = await listClientesSinLavados('2026-05-01');

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes?sinLavadosDesde=2026-05-01',
      { credentials: 'include' },
    );
    expect(data).toEqual([{ codigoPersona: 1 }]);
  });

  test('getCliente hace GET /api/clientes/:id con credentials include', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: { codigoPersona: 5 } }),
    });

    const data = await getCliente(5);

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/clientes/5', { credentials: 'include' });
    expect(data).toEqual({ codigoPersona: 5 });
  });

  test('deleteCliente hace DELETE /api/clientes/:id con credentials include', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 204 });

    await deleteCliente(5);

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/api/clientes/5', {
      method: 'DELETE',
      credentials: 'include',
    });
  });

  test('deleteCliente adjunta el estado HTTP al error', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ ok: false, status: 409 });

    await expect(deleteCliente(5)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409 });
  });

  test('updateCliente hace PUT /api/clientes/:id con credentials include', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: { codigoPersona: 5 } }),
    });

    await updateCliente(5, { nombre: 'A' });

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes/5',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'PUT', credentials: 'include' }),
    );
  });

  test('createCliente hace POST /api/clientes con credentials include', async () => {
    globalThis.fetch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      json: () => Promise.resolve({ data: { codigoPersona: 7 } }),
    });

    const data = await createCliente({ nombre: 'A' });

    expect(globalThis.fetch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      '/api/clientes',
      expect.objectContaining({ method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' }),
    );
    expect(data).toEqual({ codigoPersona: 7 });
  });
});
